**[00:20:56.560] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000255)  
and unlock vital financial capital,

**[00:20:58.700] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000256)  
we are aggressively scaling up

**[00:21:00.780] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000257)  
land titling to issue

**[00:21:01.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000258)  
43,000 land patents,

**[00:21:04.580] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000259)  
including residential, agricultural,

**[00:21:07.140] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000260)  
and special patents in 2027.

**[00:21:09.940] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000261)  
On minerals development,

**[00:21:11.880] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000262)  
we are driving toward

**[00:21:12.800] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000263)  
a 2% mining GDP

**[00:21:14.320] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000264)  
contribution by 2028,

**[00:21:16.880] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000265)  
up from 0.74%

**[00:21:18.880] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000266)  
in 2025.

**[00:21:20.560] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000267)  
We are expanding small-scale mining

**[00:21:22.500] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000268)  
opportunities through the Minahang Bayan Program

**[00:21:24.560] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000269)  
and fast-tracking 43

**[00:21:26.560] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000270)  
critical mineral quadrangle

**[00:21:28.380] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000271)  
mapping assessments,

**[00:21:30.200] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000272)  
strictly balanced with rigorous compliance monitoring and enforcement. Second, we are advancing environmental quality

**[00:21:37.240] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000273)  
and safeguarding water security.

**[00:21:39.900] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000274)  
In light of the forecasted

**[00:21:41.120] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000275)  
El Nino conditions persisting through

**[00:21:42.880] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000276)  
2027,

**[00:21:44.560] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000277)  
we are implementing proactive water supply

**[00:21:47.000] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000278)  
augmentation, infrastructure upgrades,

**[00:21:49.820] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000279)  
and critical operational

**[00:21:51.000] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000280)  
adjustments. These measures

**[00:21:53.140] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000281)  
are coordinated among key water

**[00:21:54.920] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000282)  
agencies alongside demand

**[00:21:56.440] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000283)  
management measures.

**[00:21:58.700] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000284)  
Public conservation information drives

**[00:22:00.700] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000285)  
and consumption monitoring.

**[00:22:02.700] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000286)  
We are allocating

**[00:22:05.700] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000287)  
124 million pesos

**[00:22:07.520] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000288)  
in direct financial assistance

**[00:22:09.160] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000289)  
to local water districts

**[00:22:10.500] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000290)  
to expand reliable clean water

**[00:22:12.940] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000291)  
access to isolated and

**[00:22:14.700] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000292)  
unserved barangays.

**[00:22:16.800] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000293)  
On circular economy and solid waste

**[00:22:18.580] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000294)  
management, through the Extended

**[00:22:20.540] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000295)  
Producer Responsibility Act,

**[00:22:22.640] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000296)  
we have mobilized over 1,000

**[00:22:24.460] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000297)  
obliged enterprises,

**[00:22:27.720] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000298)  
successfully diverting

**[00:22:28.700] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000299)  
244 million kilograms

**[00:22:30.940] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000300)  
of plastic packaging footprint

**[00:22:33.200] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000301)  
from terrestrial and marine ecosystems.

**[00:22:36.000] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000302)  
Additionally, we are providing direct technical

**[00:22:39.900] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000303)  
assistance to operationalize materials recovery

**[00:22:42.260] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000304)  
facilities in 4,400 barangays.

**[00:22:45.100] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000305)  
We likewise continue our aggressive cleanup

**[00:22:48.060] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000306)  
and rehabilitation of vital urban waterways,

**[00:22:51.340] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000307)  
particularly the Manila Bay, Pasig River

**[00:22:53.940] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000308)  
system, and Laguna Lake.

**[00:22:55.460] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000309)  
Third, we are reinforcing natural

**[00:22:58.700] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000310)  
defense systems and scaling

**[00:23:00.160] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000311)  
science-based climate adaptation.

**[00:23:02.860] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000312)  
Through the Mines and Geosciences Bureau,

**[00:23:04.460] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000313)  
our 24-7 Geo Hazards

**[00:23:06.460] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000314)  
Operations Center issues

**[00:23:08.440] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000315)  
real-time threat advisories

**[00:23:10.300] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000316)  
to LGUs, prioritizing

**[00:23:12.460] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000317)  
the 18 high-exposure provinces

**[00:23:14.840] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000318)  
identified under the National Adaptation Plan.

**[00:23:18.340] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000319)  
We have institutionalized

**[00:23:19.580] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000320)  
the International Union for Conservation

**[00:23:21.520] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000321)  
of Nature Global Standard for Nature-Based Solutions

**[00:23:25.900] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000322)  
under DNR Administrative Order

**[00:23:28.420] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000323)  
2026-30.

**[00:23:31.260] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000324)  
For Fiscal Year 2027,

**[00:23:33.660] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000325)  
we have allocated 28 million pesos

**[00:23:35.800] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000326)  
across four target river basins

**[00:23:37.720] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000327)  
for flood mitigation

**[00:23:39.360] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000328)  
and 10 million to update

**[00:23:41.140] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000329)  
integrated river basin master plans

**[00:23:43.880] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000330)  
for the Buayan, Malungon,

**[00:23:46.220] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000331)  
Agno, and Pampanga River Basins.

**[00:23:49.360] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000332)  
We are strengthening active management

**[00:23:51.020] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000333)  
across 121 legislated protected areas

**[00:23:54.180] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000334)  
covering 4.51 million hectares

**[00:23:56.880] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000335)  
and expanding marine protected areas.

**[00:23:58.340] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000336)  
Underpinning all of these strategic priorities

**[00:24:03.620] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000337)  
is the institutional shift brought about

**[00:24:05.440] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000338)  
by the PENCAS law.

**[00:24:07.160] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000339)  
By establishing site-specific ecosystem accounts,

**[00:24:11.060] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000340)  
we are assigning tangible economic value

**[00:24:14.000] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000341)  
to nature services,

**[00:24:15.520] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000342)  
ensuring data-driven planning and resource allocation.

**[00:24:19.480] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000343)  
To conclude, Madam Chair and honorable members,

**[00:24:22.100] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000344)  
environment and financing is a high return investment

**[00:24:25.340] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000345)  
in nature-positive, climate-resilient,

**[00:24:28.220] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000346)  
and sustainable Filipino communities and livelihoods.

**[00:24:30.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000347)  
We respectfully request your full support

**[00:24:32.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000348)  
in passing the DNR Fiscal Year 2027 budget.

**[00:24:36.440] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000349)  
Thank you very much, Madam Chair.

**[00:26:58.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000418)  
Before I turn over to Undersecretary Anate, Madam Chair,

**[00:27:03.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000419)  
just to give you an idea of the different measures and efforts

**[00:27:08.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000420)  
that have been exerted to address the upcoming Super El Niño.

**[00:27:13.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000421)  
For here in the NCR region, Madam Chair,

**[00:27:18.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000422)  
of course, we're working with MWSS,

**[00:27:21.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000423)  
with the two water concessionaires,

**[00:27:22.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000424)  
and there are 61 newly re-energized deep wells

**[00:27:33.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000425)  
that are already operational.

**[00:27:36.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000426)  
As far as stationary tanks are concerned,

**[00:27:38.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000427)  
there are 135.

**[00:27:40.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000428)  
For mobile tankers, 182.

**[00:27:43.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000429)  
And then the DNR has about 15 units

**[00:27:49.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000430)  
of what we call the,

**[00:27:51.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000431)  
water bladders donated by the Netherlands government

**[00:27:58.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000432)  
that we're also stationing in strategic areas

**[00:28:01.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000433)  
that may need water supply eventually

**[00:28:07.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000434)  
if the case reaches the worst scenario.

**[00:28:11.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000435)  
Then of course,

**[00:28:13.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000436)  
there's also going to be regulating of pressure management

**[00:28:18.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000437)  
to minimize this.

**[00:28:21.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000438)  
To conserve water.

**[00:28:23.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000439)  
I also sent to the Office of the President,

**[00:28:28.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000440)  
Madam Chair,

**[00:28:29.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000441)  
a possible memorandum circular

**[00:28:32.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000442)  
to be issued by the President

**[00:28:35.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000443)  
to at least direct and mandate

**[00:28:38.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000444)  
all government offices

**[00:28:41.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000445)  
and instrumentalities of government

**[00:28:44.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000446)  
to observe strict conservation measures.

**[00:28:48.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000447)  
Because,

**[00:28:49.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000448)  
as maybe later,

**[00:28:52.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000449)  
MWSS and LUA can make a statement on this

**[00:28:59.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000450)  
regarding non-revenue water,

**[00:29:01.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000451)  
which means yung mga leakage po natin,

**[00:29:04.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000452)  
there's quite a big percentage of this,

**[00:29:06.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000453)  
which we would like to minimize,

**[00:29:08.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000454)  
and one way would be for instance

**[00:29:10.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000455)  
for at least government offices

**[00:29:12.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000456)  
to close the valves of the main water sources

**[00:29:17.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000457)  
in the different buildings.

**[00:29:18.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000458)  
At a certain time

**[00:29:21.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000459)  
during the night,

**[00:29:24.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000460)  
Madam Chair.

**[00:29:25.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000461)  
If you will allow me, Madam Chair,

**[00:29:27.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000462)  
I'll pass on to Undersecretary Anate

**[00:29:29.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000463)  
for the specifics on the figures.

And later,

**[00:29:33.980] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000466)  
let's follow up that point about

**[00:29:34.980] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000467)  
saan kailangan tayo maghigpit

**[00:29:37.980] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000468)  
para the essential water supply

**[00:29:39.980] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000469)  
will still reach people.

**[00:29:41.980] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000470)  
But of course,

**[00:29:42.980] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000471)  
kailangan italakay niyo yun

**[00:29:44.980] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000472)  
in a comprehensive program

**[00:29:46.980] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000473)  
para hindi mag-panic ang

**[00:29:47.980] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000474)  
kung sinong kaagad

**[00:29:49.980] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000475)  
kaagad maapektuhan,

**[00:29:51.980] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000476)  
pero maintindihan

**[00:29:52.980] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000477)  
bakit kailangan natin i-prioritize

**[00:29:55.980] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000478)  
certain water needs of people

**[00:29:58.980] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000479)  
just so we can...

**[00:30:00.000] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000480)  
stretch out the existing supply at umabot tayo, no, past the Super El Nino.

**[00:30:05.140] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000481)  
Yes, Yusek Analiza, please.

**[00:30:07.220] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000482)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[00:30:08.800] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000483)  
Madam Chair, if we will consider the other programs, projects, and activities within the department,

**[00:30:15.240] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000484)  
we can, by attribution, we can say that there's about 1.7 billion pesos

**[00:30:20.500] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000485)  
that can actually be considered as nature-based solutions.

**[00:30:25.100] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000486)  
So, this one is from Forest Development, Rehabilitation, Maintenance, and Protection,

**[00:30:31.580] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000487)  
where we implement enhanced planting, assisted natural generation, reforestation, agroforestry,

**[00:30:38.360] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000488)  
and this is also where maintenance and protection for the National Greening Program is included.

**[00:30:43.920] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000489)  
The second one is with respect to soil conservation,

**[00:30:48.060] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000490)  
where we undertake, we plan to undertake the scaling up of nature-based solutions

**[00:30:53.460] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000491)  
for flood risk reduction.

**[00:30:55.100] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000492)  
Then, water resource management.

**[00:30:56.860] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000493)  
This is, Madam Chair, the one that you mentioned about the 28 million pesos.

**[00:31:01.420] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000494)  
And then, establishment of vegetative messures.

**[00:31:04.900] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000495)  
We also consider the maintenance and protection of about 7,000 hectares in the protected areas

**[00:31:12.680] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000496)  
as part of our nature-based solutions.

**[00:31:15.940] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000497)  
Support to livelihood, such as social enterprise development for NGP products

**[00:31:21.820] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000498)  
and biodiversity-friendly enterprise under the Coastal.

**[00:31:25.100] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000499)  
And marine are also considered as part of the nature-based solutions.

**[00:31:31.440] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000500)  
And then, the soil conservation primarily to form, strengthen, or operationalize

**[00:31:37.200] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000501)  
the 54 Watershed Mitigation Councils.

**[00:31:41.140] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000502)  
In terms of enforcement, Madam Chair, we also included here

**[00:31:44.620] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000503)  
LAWIN Forest and Biodiversity Protection System.

**[00:31:49.120] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000504)  
So, in total, there's about 1.7 billion pesos, Madam Chair.

**[00:31:55.100] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000505)  
So, 1.7 billion out of the total requested, as it stands now, 28 billion.

**[00:32:00.560] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000506)  
So, that's like 5% of the whole department budget,

**[00:32:04.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000507)  
particularly for nature-based solutions.

**[00:32:06.700] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000508)  
Itong total amount ba so far for nature-based solutions,

**[00:32:11.080] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000509)  
pareho na rin ito dun sa kabuo ang halaga na nakalaan para sa Super El Nino?

**[00:32:18.440] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000510)  
Or magkakaiba yun? Or may overlap ba yun?

**[00:32:21.740] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000511)  
May overlap, Madam Chair.

**[00:32:23.320] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000512)  
We still have to...

**[00:32:25.100] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000513)  
segregate exactly what will be allocated for the very strong El Nino, Madam Chair.

**[00:32:34.400] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000514)  
Could you also give us, Usec, the total amount so far

**[00:32:37.680] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000515)  
ng lahat naman ang mga items na nakafocus sa paghahanda at pagtugon sa Super El Nino?

**[00:32:44.720] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000516)  
Yes, Sec Cuna.

**[00:32:46.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000517)  
Oh, I'm sorry.

**[00:32:48.980] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000518)  
Yes, Madam Chair, we'll look at the specific figures for the actions

**[00:32:55.100] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000519)  
just for the Super El Nino.

**[00:32:58.040] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000520)  
What we intend to do, Madam Chair, is to overlay the areas

**[00:33:04.320] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000521)  
where the very strong Super El Nino will strike,

**[00:33:08.780] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000522)  
overlay it with the areas where there is a strong groundwater stress areas also,

**[00:33:16.360] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000523)  
and where the areas where assistance for water supply is being given.

**[00:33:25.100] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000524)  
So that we can prioritize which areas, the impact areas, Madam Chair,

**[00:33:29.200] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000525)  
and from there, we will be able to determine the specific costs that will be just allocated.

**[00:33:34.600] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000526)  
So, Madam Chair, we will submit the figures there.

**[00:33:37.200] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000527)  
Please do so within this hearing.

**[00:33:39.380] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000528)  
Kasi yun actually yung follow-up question ko rin tungkol sa areas na ma-identify nyo

**[00:33:45.480] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000529)  
by overlaying pinaka-vulnerable, pinaka may malalim naman na groundwater,

**[00:33:49.500] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000530)  
at pinaka mangangailangan ng assistance.

**[00:33:52.240] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000531)  
And importanteng masagot nyo kami kung...

**[00:33:55.100] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000532)  
magkano kabuan na ang nakalaan para sa Super El Nino.

**[00:33:58.800] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000533)  
Kasi para masagot nyo rin yung tanong ko kanina na

**[00:34:02.380] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000534)  
aling mga programa ang kailangan pang dagdagan ngayon.

**[00:34:06.120] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000535)  
But before I go to my next question,

**[00:34:08.180] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000536)  
the Chair would like to acknowledge the presence of Senator Pia.

**[00:34:11.460] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000537)  
Thank you for joining us.

**[00:34:12.560] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000538)  
Would you also like to make an opening statement before we continue?

**[00:34:19.620] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000539)  
Good morning.

**[00:34:21.020] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000540)  
Good morning, Secretary, and good morning, everyone.

**[00:34:25.100] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000541)

**[00:34:29.300] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000542)  
Taas ang kamay.

**[00:34:31.600] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000543)  
Marami?

**[00:34:33.080] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000544)  
Marami kayo, no?

**[00:34:34.060] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000545)  
Because, Secretary, if I...

**[00:34:36.720] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000546)  
Do I remember correctly, when I was Chairman of the Committee on Environment,

**[00:34:41.680] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000547)  
EMB ka ba?

**[00:34:43.320] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000548)  
O, see?

**[00:34:43.880] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000549)  
Yes.

**[00:34:44.560] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000550)  
Umaandar pa yung aking memory.

**[00:34:46.740] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000551)  
Because we did a lot of work on that, and I think even the passing of the NIPAS law.

**[00:34:50.840] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000552)  
But I know nga maraming matatagal na dyan.

**[00:34:53.180] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000553)  
So, thank you for your work.

**[00:34:54.340] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000554)  
There's a lot of work.

**[00:34:54.940] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000555)  
There's a lot of work ahead of us.

**[00:34:56.100] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000556)  
I just want to mention that in 2008, I authored and sponsored the Environmental Education Act.

**[00:35:05.860] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000557)  
And I think it is...

**[00:35:08.240] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000558)  
I thought it was very relevant then.

**[00:35:10.300] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000559)  
I think it's even as relevant now because I'm preaching to the choir.

**[00:35:16.920] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000560)  
So, you all know how important that is.

**[00:35:18.800] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000561)  
And we just had our budget hearing with DepEd.

**[00:35:22.840] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000562)  
And I will personally send a message to Secretary Angara about this because it tasked DepEd and CHED to include it in their curriculum with the guidance of DNR.

**[00:35:40.040] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000563)  
And the law is a very simple law, but the law specifically recognizes that every geographical area is different.

**[00:35:47.180] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000564)  
So, obviously, yung mga barangay na malapit sa dagat,

**[00:35:51.060] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000565)  
edi syempre, yung marine protection ang pinaka-importante sa kanila.

**[00:35:54.840] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000566)  
And then, yung nasa bundok naman, yung sa bundok.

**[00:35:56.840] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000567)  
And then, for all of our young people, because they're the ones who influence the older people,

**[00:36:03.320] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000568)  
recognize how this all affects all their lives.

**[00:36:08.240] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000569)  
So, I think it's a good time to review where we are at that.

**[00:36:12.460] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000570)  
And to be clear...

**[00:36:14.040] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000571)  
Bakit?

**[00:36:15.120] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000572)  
Same ba? Sorry, same ba?

**[00:36:17.600] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000573)  
Mas may detail.

**[00:36:18.560] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000574)  
Normally iba ka, di ba?

**[00:36:21.060] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000575)  
Anyway, hindi ko alam. Sorry.

**[00:36:25.840] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000576)  
Mas may detail yung sa 'yo

**[00:36:27.240] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000577)  
Okay.

**[00:36:29.480] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000578)  
So, what I wanted to emphasize now is that we recognize that different geographies are affected differently.

**[00:36:38.880] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000579)  
And it's time that we really include it in our children's education.

**[00:36:43.520] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000580)  
But what I want to emphasize is I also believe that legislators should not...

**[00:36:51.060] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000581)  
intervene and micromanage the curriculum.

**[00:36:54.360] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000582)  
So, hindi ko sinasabi yun.

**[00:36:56.040] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000583)  
Ito yung laging medyo mahirap intindihin unless you understand how these curriculums are developed.

**[00:37:02.600] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000584)  
It's embedded in their different subjects.

**[00:37:06.180] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000585)  
Okay naman ako dyan, Secretary.

**[00:37:07.720] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000586)  
So, maybe you can just assign.

**[00:37:09.580] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000587)  
Is there a specific division that would be in charge of that?

**[00:37:13.940] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000588)  
There is. Okay, later na lang.

**[00:37:14.960] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000589)  
Because anyway, this is just my introductory statement.

**[00:37:16.700] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000590)  
So, and then, of course, the committee I chair is the Sustainable Development Goals.

**[00:37:21.040] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000591)  
Innovation Futures Thinking.

**[00:37:22.760] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000592)  
And there's a lot of SDGs that fall under your direction and need your specific input.

**[00:37:33.000] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000593)  
So, maybe somebody can also discuss that.

**[00:37:36.180] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000594)  
Because the problem with SDGs is they are so overlapping that sometimes no one's in charge.

**[00:37:43.080] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000595)  
And that's really what I want to dive into later on.

**[00:37:47.740] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000596)  
Obviously, yung responsible consumption.

**[00:37:51.040] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000597)  
SDG 12.

**[00:37:52.780] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000598)  
I think inyo talaga yun, no?

**[00:37:54.580] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000599)  
Yung mga tetrapak, yung mga sachets.

**[00:37:58.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000600)  
But there's others that become part of your responsibility.

**[00:38:04.080] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000601)  
Pero hindi siguro natin napipinpoint.

**[00:38:06.560] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000602)  
So, I'll go into that later on when I have my own time.

**[00:38:08.840] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000603)  
Thank you for now.

**[00:38:09.900] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000604)  
We can proceed, Madam Chairperson.

**[00:38:12.580] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000605)  
Thank you.

**[00:38:13.020] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000606)  
Thank you, Senator Pia.

**[00:38:14.420] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000607)  
Madam Chair, if I may?

**[00:38:15.300] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000608)  
Yes, Eko na.

**[00:38:16.160] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000609)  
Just to follow up on what the good Senator mentioned.

**[00:38:19.680] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000610)  
Because what Senator Allen brought up earlier, the importance of the education, environmental education.

**[00:38:26.940] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000611)  
I was going to mention that there was a law passed already in 2008, Environmental Awareness and Education Act.

**[00:38:33.700] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000612)  
But I think we need to collaborate with both CHED and DepEd on more detailed modules that will be integrated in the curriculum now.

**[00:38:48.080] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000613)  
Both for...

**[00:38:49.680] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000614)  
For elementary and even higher.

**[00:38:51.840] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000615)  
So that it will be, you know, inculcated in all our students.

**[00:38:55.540] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000616)  
And what should be, by nature, the behavior, what it should actually be in terms of solid waste management and other aspects.

**[00:39:07.560] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000617)  
Salamat, Sec.

**[00:39:08.300] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000618)  
Maybe while we're at it, you could also include engaging TESDA about this.

**[00:39:12.400] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000619)  
Okay.

**[00:39:13.000] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000620)  
Salamat, Sec.

**[00:39:13.740] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000621)  
So, back to you, Sec.

**[00:39:15.740] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000622)  
Analiza.

**[00:39:16.600] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000623)  
Kanina binanggit nyo po na i-overlay nyo yung mga mapa.

**[00:39:19.680] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000624)  
Para ma-identify yung mga area na pinakamangangailangan ng tulong, may pinakamalalim na groundwater, tapos yung pinaka-vulnerable.

**[00:39:29.300] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000625)  
So, follow up dun, na-identify nyo po ba yung mga probinsya at regyon na pinakamaapektuhan, bahagya lamang, or hardly at all, or pinakamatindi yung katamtaman, tapos bahagya lamang, ng Super El Nino.

**[00:39:46.600] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000626)  
At least, simula noong 2027.

**[00:39:50.720] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000627)  
Yes, Madam Chair.

**[00:39:51.880] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000628)  
Ang pag-asa po ang nag-release ng data, kung sino yung very high impact, moderate impact.

**[00:39:59.520] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000629)  
So, yun po yung pinaka-baseline namin sa mapa na nagagawin.

**[00:40:04.620] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000630)  
And with that pag-asa baseline, nagko-coordinate din ba kayo, halimbawa, sa Philippine Space Agency, Project NOAA, pati yung UP Resilience Institute,

**[00:40:14.980] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000631)  
para matuko at mabantayan yung mga lugar na nanganganib sa tagtuyot?

**[00:40:21.020] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000632)  
Yes, Madam Chair. There's a task force, El Nino, under the OCD and the RRMC. So, doon po yung sharing of data, Madam Chair.

**[00:40:31.760] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000633)  
And lahat po nitong mga ahensya, kasama po, kasabay ng DNR.

**[00:40:36.620] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000634)  
And may malinaw na plano na ba para maunang matulungan yung mga lugar na yan?

**[00:40:44.200] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000635)  
According to the severity of vulnerability and probable impact.

**[00:40:49.080] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000636)  
Yes, Madam Chair. OCD already has some drafts. They call it SNAP. I forgot the meaning of the acronym.

**[00:40:55.460] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000637)  
But DNR has also drafted its own very strong El Nino mitigation response plans.

**[00:41:05.180] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000638)  
And we have a proposed directive already to be signed by the Secretary for that, Madam Chair.

**[00:41:11.120] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000639)  
Directive signed by the Secretary.

**[00:41:12.600] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000640)  
Bukod pa po dun sa...

**[00:41:14.200] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000641)  
MC na binanggit nila na pinadala sa OP.

**[00:41:17.780] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000642)  
Yes, Madam Chair. Yung plano po namin na internal sa DNR will not only cover water management and conservation governance,

**[00:41:27.480] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000643)  
which will include the alternative water sources and harvesting, groundwater and salinity safeguards,

**[00:41:34.040] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000644)  
and also reservoir and dam level management.

**[00:41:36.860] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000645)  
That mitigation plan will also cover wildfire and grass fire prevention and preparedness.

**[00:41:44.200] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000646)  
Mga maalala nyo, Madam Chair, in the past, nagsara tayo ng mga PA from the tourists and mga hikers because of the forest fires.

**[00:41:53.060] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000647)  
So kasama po dito yung mga early warning and hotspot tracking natin, strict ban on open burning,

**[00:42:00.780] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000648)  
and kasama din po yung pag-monitor na natin ang open dam site and sanitary land fire prevention, fire contingency planning,

**[00:42:10.520] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000649)  
kasama din po ang flood risk prevention and management.

**[00:42:13.400] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000650)  
Kasi hindi po ibig sabihin.

**[00:42:14.040] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000651)  
Ibig sabihin na very strong elmineo ay kagtuyot lang po, but it could also draw a very strong rain.

**[00:42:23.640] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000652)  
So sasama po natin sa directive na i-issuehan po ni Secretary will be drainage and waterway desilting.

**[00:42:32.220] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000653)  
Also early warning systems, river channel improvement,

**[00:42:36.540] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000654)  
and then yung environmental and ecosystem-based adaptation,

**[00:42:41.160] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000655)  
which will include the continuation of the...

**[00:42:44.040] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000656)  
groundwater resource and vulnerability assessment of MGB and NWRB.

**[00:42:48.420] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000657)  
Of course, NGP, protected area management, and biodiversity and marine monitoring, Madam Chair.

**[00:42:55.080] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-000658)  
So yung buong ecosystem and biodiversity po ang itatakal ng mitigation plan, not just water security, Madam Chair.

**[00:43:02.060] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000659)  
So I hope, Yusek, before the hearing is over for today, makal ninyo lahat ng items, nung lahat ng iyon,

**[00:43:08.120] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000660)  
kasi yun siguro yung magbubuo ng suma total ng pinopropose so far para sa ELNC.

**[00:43:14.040] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000661)  
Makal ninyo preparation and response.

**[00:43:17.420] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000662)  
And then you can tell us at that point kung alin pa dito ang kulang ng pondo.

**[00:43:23.400] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000663)  
Now, moving on to another question.

**[00:43:25.960] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000664)  
Bawas ng P550 million ang pondo ng National Greening Program

**[00:43:31.280] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000665)  
dahil di umano ahead of schedule ang programa sa target na forest cover na 7.5 million hectares by 2028.

**[00:43:40.200] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000666)  
Kung kinal po yun mula sa NGP,

**[00:43:44.040] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000667)  
posible bang gamitin muna yan, i-repurpose, pandagdag dun sa El Nino plans ng department?

**[00:43:55.500] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000668)  
You mean, Madam Chair, if I may, you mean would it be practical to use it for this?

**[00:44:03.420] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000669)  
I believe so, Madam Chair.

**[00:44:05.140] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000670)  
One of the other efforts that we are looking at is also to assess the decommissioning,

**[00:44:14.040] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000671)  
the decommissioned deep wells that have been decommissioned in the past

**[00:44:17.620] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000672)  
and to be able to re-energize it kasi these are in many different areas in the country.

**[00:44:24.180] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000673)  
That's one.

**[00:44:25.320] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000674)  
And then also, you mentioned, Madam Chair, earlier about small water impounding systems

**[00:44:30.880] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000675)  
and we have over 2,000 around the country which are actually for the National Greening Program

**[00:44:36.800] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000676)  
that can be repurposed for domestic use at least.

**[00:44:42.120] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000677)  
And although...

**[00:44:44.040] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000678)  
So, as far as for drinking, we would have to check the water quality first

**[00:44:49.720] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000679)  
but at least for domestic use, we can repurpose that, Madam Chair.

**[00:44:56.880] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000680)  
And then, you started to mention something about this...

**[00:45:00.000] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000681)

**[00:45:02.200] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000682)

**[00:45:03.340] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000683)

**[00:45:06.060] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000684)

**[00:45:07.860] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000685)

**[00:45:10.100] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000686)

**[00:45:11.860] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000687)

**[00:45:13.900] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000688)

**[00:45:15.920] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000689)

**[00:45:18.120] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000690)

**[00:45:21.720] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000691)  
So,

**[00:45:22.280] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000692)  
Administrator Salonga.

Madam Chair, Mr. Secretary,

**[00:45:25.900] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-000694)  
yes ma'am, nagpalabas na po ng

**[00:45:27.940] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-000695)

**[00:45:29.880] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-000696)

**[00:45:31.960] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-000697)

**[00:45:33.940] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-000698)

**[00:45:36.100] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-000699)

**[00:45:37.760] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-000700)

**[00:45:40.020] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-000701)

**[00:45:41.920] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-000702)

**[00:45:44.000] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-000703)

**[00:45:46.060] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-000704)

**[00:45:48.140] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-000705)

**[00:45:50.300] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000706)

**[00:45:56.620] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000709)

**[00:45:59.760] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000711)

**[00:46:01.980] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000712)

**[00:46:03.700] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000713)

**[00:46:05.700] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000714)

**[00:46:07.600] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000715)

**[00:46:17.800] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-000720)

**[00:46:19.580] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-000721)

**[00:46:21.840] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-000722)

**[00:46:25.380] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-000724)

**[00:46:29.760] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-000725)

**[00:46:37.040] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-000729)

**[00:46:39.200] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000730)

**[00:46:45.140] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-000733)

**[00:46:47.400] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-000734)

**[00:46:50.340] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-000735)  
Gusto nga namin gawin

**[00:46:51.640] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-000736)

So government will set

**[00:46:55.540] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000738)

**[00:46:57.200] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000739)

**[00:47:01.040] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000741)

**[00:47:07.400] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000743)

Okay.

**[00:47:12.000] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000745)

**[00:47:15.040] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000747)

**[00:47:20.560] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000750)

**[00:47:22.820] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-000751)

**[01:02:08.840] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001198)

**[01:02:33.560] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001202)

**[01:02:41.480] FMB Director Arturo E. Fadriquela** (asr-001203)  
Maintained lang. Maintained lang. Maintained lang ang ating sa forest protection.

**[01:02:48.660] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001204)  
Maintained lang.

**[01:02:49.320] FMB Director Arturo E. Fadriquela** (asr-001205)

**[01:02:55.600] FMB Director Arturo E. Fadriquela** (asr-001206)  
At mag-a-

**[01:02:56.260] FMB Director Arturo E. Fadriquela** (asr-001207)

**[01:03:06.220] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001209)

**[01:03:21.360] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001210)

**[01:03:25.340] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001211)

**[01:03:49.760] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001215)

**[01:04:22.380] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001222)

**[01:04:26.180] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001223)

**[01:04:28.840] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001224)

Noong taong 2027.

**[01:06:31.960] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001249)  
If we have the funding Madam Chair, we can add to this.

**[01:06:36.880] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001250)  
From 13, ilan ang optimal at efficient number na dapat merong ang department?

**[01:06:50.560] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001251)  
Sir Secretary, Madam Chair.

**[01:06:53.820] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001252)  
Ma'am, if I may suggest.

**[01:06:55.720] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001253)  
Yes, Admin Salonga.

**[01:06:56.920] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001254)  
Kahit 50.

**[01:06:58.840] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001255)  
50 po. Para we can replace them immediately in large water districts for deployment in areas na talagang wala.

**[01:07:08.820] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001256)  
Nandiyan na ba yan sa proposed budget ng department sa line item na yun?

**[01:07:13.560] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001257)  
Sa luwa po.

**[01:07:14.660] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001258)  
Sa luwa?

**[01:07:15.520] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001259)  
Opo. Bibigay na lang po namin sa inyo.

**[01:07:17.720] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001260)  
Okay. So wala pa dun sa ngayon?

**[01:07:19.760] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001261)  
Okay. So this is times 4 ng current na meron na 13.

**[01:07:25.960] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001262)  
Okay.

**[01:07:28.840] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001263)  
Then, syempre, nasa ilalim nyo yung mga water districts.

**[01:07:31.520] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001264)  
Paano nyo ipopromote yung collaboration sa pagitan ng mga water districts?

**[01:07:36.560] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001265)  
Lalo na yung mga adjacent, yung mga magkakatabi.

**[01:07:40.060] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001266)  
Limbawa, may sapat na tubig yung isang water district pero nagkukulang naman yung katabing lugar, yung adjacent na lugar.

**[01:07:47.980] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001267)  
Paano nyo sila matutulungan magbahagi ng supply sa isa't isa?

**[01:07:52.900] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001268)  
Madam Chair, Mr. Secretary, actually yun po maganda dun sa water district regime natin.

**[01:07:57.720] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001269)  
Talagang.

**[01:07:58.840] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001270)  
In the 53 years ng luwa, nagkaroon sila ng parang coordination amongst their clustered water districts.

**[01:08:05.160] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001271)  
So talagang nagtutulungan sila.

**[01:08:07.060] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001272)  
Even in events of travesty or merong sakuna dun, ang unang response ng water district is from the neighboring water district.

**[01:08:17.660] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001273)  
Bago before big government moves in, yung mga magkakatabing water districts ang nagtutulungan.

**[01:08:24.040] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001274)  
So we can actually pre-place them.

**[01:08:25.880] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001275)  
Saka hindi naman ako nababahala na hindi sila magtutulungan.

**[01:08:28.840] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001276)  
Talagang nagtutulungan po sila.

**[01:08:33.600] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001277)  
So walang kailangan kayong ipromote? May reflex na, may natural behavior na sila?

**[01:08:40.760] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001278)  
Or meron pa ba kayong idadagdag na pako-ordination sa kanila sa harap ng Super El Nino?

**[01:08:47.500] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001279)  
Madam Chair, nandun na po sa memorandum circular po yun. Tapos clustered po sila.

**[01:08:51.700] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001280)  
Meron rin sila mga regional organization ng water districts.

**[01:08:55.000] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001281)  
So madali silang makapag-move, communicate, and help each other.

**[01:08:58.840] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001282)  
Other in times of tragedy.

**[01:09:00.900] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001283)  
Balik na rin naman natin, paano nyo i-handle kung magkaroon ng conflict o kaya disputes sa pagitan nitong mga water districts?

**[01:09:10.400] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001284)  
Pati LGUs, no?

**[01:09:11.880] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001285)  
Yung mga nag-host ng water sources at yung mga LGU na purely consumer lang ng tubig.

**[01:09:19.320] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001286)  
Parang writ small yung sinasabing tension lagi sa, kunyari, mga regyon sa mundo tulad ng Middle East na tubig talaga ang pinakamahalaga

**[01:09:28.180] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001287)  
at maaring pag-awayang resource.

**[01:09:30.020] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001288)  
Madam Chair, nakikita namin yan. Pero yung water allocation is actually not a distribution issue.

**[01:09:36.300] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001289)  
It's more of an allocation dito galing po sa NWRB.

**[01:09:39.720] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001290)  
But nakikita na namin yan ngayon kasi for example, there are some metro water districts that take water from other cities

**[01:09:48.260] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001291)  
and yung local city that hosts the water source want to de-annex to preserve their own water source.

**[01:09:54.100] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001292)  
So meron na pong conflicts na ganyan nangyayari.

**[01:09:56.820] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001293)  
Kasi sinusubukan na lang naming ayusin by ensuring that the host water, the host LGU that has the water is first satisfied doon sa demands nila.

**[01:10:09.520] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001294)  
At that level, kaya ng luwa. Pero yung allocation po mismo ng water source, hindi po sa amin doon.

**[01:10:15.540] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001295)  
Baka sometimes during this hearing, gustong mag-weigh in ang NWRB tungkol dito.

**[01:10:23.780] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001296)  
Paano pinipreempt or nire-resolve?

**[01:10:25.940] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001297)  
Kapag nga magkaroon ng ganitong conflict or disputes.

**[01:10:30.280] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001298)  
Would you like to input something at this point, Dr. David?

**[01:10:33.240] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-001299)  
Yes, ma'am. Good afternoon, Madam Chair. With the permission of the Secretary.

**[01:10:38.000] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-001300)  
Yes, Madam Chair. In terms of allocation and water rights, ang NWRB po is with the authority and quasi-judicial function

**[01:10:47.580] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-001301)  
to resolve po yung mga conflicts between bar use, water use.

**[01:10:51.700] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-001302)  
But of course, as per water code po, Madam Chair,

**[01:10:55.940] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-001303)  
priority po yung domestic and household use in the evaluation and assessment of water use or water rights.

**[01:11:05.160] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-001304)  
At of course, tinitingnan po natin din dyan yung availability ng tubig po for a certain area kung saan po nagkakaroon ng conflict.

**[01:11:16.520] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-001305)  
But of course, with the condition that must priority po yung domestic and household use over irrigation and of course power and other.

**[01:11:25.940] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001306)  
Sa ngayon po, Dr. David, may bilang na ba kayo ng mga magkakatabi or adjacent na water districts na mayroon or magkakaroon ng ganitong dispute o conflict?

**[01:11:38.480] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001307)  
So between isang water-resourced at yung isang water-dependent na water district or LGU?

**[01:11:45.660] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-001308)  
Yes po, Madam Chair. Meron po kaming mga listing ng potential conflict.

**[01:11:51.740] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-001309)  
But usually po, di naman between water district.

**[01:11:55.940] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-001310)  
Actually po, between water district and the local government unit or some water service providers po.

**[01:12:02.660] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001311)  
Mga ilan yung ganitong dispute or conflict areas all over the country?

**[01:12:08.020] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-001312)  
If with the provision of the Chair, pwede po namin i-provide yung list later on.

**[01:12:13.620] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001313)  
Please do. Either during this hearing or bago nung plenary.

**[01:12:16.700] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001314)  
And in general, kung ilan man ang mga ito na may actual or potential conflict, may sapat na panahon ba?

**[01:12:25.460] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001315)  
Ang NWRB para i-resolve ito bago tumama yung pinakamalakas na epekto ng Super El Nino?

**[01:12:32.040] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-001316)  
Ang strategy po na Madam Chair ngayon is to resolve yung mga conflict kung saan po yung mga water stress.

**[01:12:37.640] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-001317)  
You prioritize po kung saan po yung mga water stress areas.

**[01:12:41.900] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001318)  
Priority nyo. Pero hindi ko matansya yung scale kasi bibigay nyo pa lang sa subcommittee namin yung bilang.

**[01:12:50.780] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001319)  
Pero kakayanin nyo po ba ma-resolve itong mga priority?

**[01:12:55.460] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001320)  
Conflict or dispute areas bago pa nga yung kasagsagan ng Super El Nino?

**[01:13:02.180] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-001321)  
We will tingnan po muna namin yung timeline, Madam Chair.

**[01:13:09.760] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001322)  
Sige po. Pahingin na lang nung data na yun para matignan din yung subcommittee namin.

**[01:13:14.460] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001323)  
And then, last sa paksang ito, nakausap nyo na po ba ang DPWH?

**[01:13:23.180] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001324)  
Whether NWRB or LUA?

**[01:13:25.460] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001325)  
Usap ba ang DP ukol sa pagtap ng pondo para sa level 1 water supply system since yan po ang hawak ng DP?

**[01:13:34.920] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001326)  
Yes, Administrator Salonga.

**[01:13:40.900] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001328)

**[01:13:47.060] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001331)

Paki-update na lang yung subcommittee namin tungkol dyan.

**[01:13:55.460] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001333)  
Before we move on to the MWSSRO, just a few follow-up questions.

**[01:14:04.760] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001334)  
Ayan, bago lang magpatuloy.

**[01:14:38.780] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001341)  
Madam Chair, if I may add, still on the same topic on El Nino.

**[01:14:43.400] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001342)  
We have, through an executive order in 2023 from the President, the Water Resource Management Office was created.

**[01:14:52.480] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001343)  
Supposedly, it was a precursor for the Department of Water, Madam Chair.

**[01:15:00.000] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001355)  
So this is under the DENR, which is why we have integrated the other water-related offices under this WRMO, namely NWRB.

**[01:15:00.120] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001344)  
...

**[01:15:13.540] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001356)  
Well, NWRB has been our attached agency since time immemorial, but the LUA and the MWSS, kasama na rin po namin.

**[01:15:22.800] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001357)  
But under the WRMO itself, they have some proposed projects also for next year.

**[01:15:30.520] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001358)  
But actually, they're existing projects and there are some projects proposed for next year.

**[01:15:35.040] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001359)  
These are specifically water filtration program, desalination.

**[01:15:40.860] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001360)  
These are small infrastructure, small structures, Madam Chair.

**[01:15:46.340] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001361)  
Water filtration for fresh water and then refilling stations, water tanks.

**[01:15:52.120] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001362)  
For water districts or LGUs.

**[01:15:54.260] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001363)  
And then yung geo-resistivity survey, Madam Chair, which is essentially like exploration for more water resources.

**[01:16:03.360] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001364)  
So there's a budget proposed for this.

**[01:16:05.840] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001365)  
It's 223,064,000 total for these different.

**[01:16:12.420] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001366)  
There are existing projects already, Madam Chair, which I have actually personally visited a few of them.

**[01:16:17.920] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001367)  
And they're very beneficial.

**[01:16:20.920] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001368)  
Beneficial in the sense that most of these identified areas we chose were those which were very hard to reach or underserved talaga in terms of access to water, Madam Chair.

**[01:16:36.860] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001369)  
And SEC, itong 223,000,000 para sa lahat ng klase ng mga small water impounding systems na ito.

**[01:16:43.880] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001370)  
Kasama na po ba ito dun sa...

**[01:16:46.020] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001371)  
Madam Chair, if I may.

**[01:16:46.880] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001372)  
Hindi ko mga...

**[01:16:48.460] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001373)  
Hindi ko mga Swiss ito.

**[01:16:49.080] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001374)  
Sorry.

**[01:16:49.560] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001375)  
Water filtration program.

**[01:16:50.920] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001376)  
Programs yung iba.

**[01:16:53.060] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001377)  
Summer desalination.

**[01:16:54.480] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001378)  
Yung iba, fresh water.

**[01:16:55.420] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001379)  
Para lang maging potable to the people.

**[01:16:57.960] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001380)  
The access lang.

**[01:16:58.700] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001381)  
Because many of these are in island communities, island barangays, or really far-flung areas that people have to travel very far to get access to water.

**[01:17:10.120] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001382)  
So there are certain areas that we pinpointed that we put up these projects, Madam Chair.

**[01:17:19.560] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001383)  
Salamat, SEC.

**[01:17:20.920] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001384)  
So before we go on to the MWRO, a question actually na nabanggit din kanina about, I think it was the Secretary, about Manila Water, Maynilad, for example.

**[01:17:34.440] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001385)  
Paalala lang, SEC or USEC Annalisa, to please prepare the data for the subcommittee on the most vulnerable areas.

**[01:17:43.760] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001386)  
Most, middling, and least vulnerable to Super El Nino.

**[01:17:47.920] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001387)  
And kung handa na rin kayo at that point with the...

**[01:17:50.760] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001388)  
Overlay map para ma-read into the record yung mga areas most vulnerable dun sa Super El Nino.

**[01:17:58.440] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001389)  
Are you ready already now, USEC Annalisa, or shall, in a bit?

**[01:18:04.640] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001390)  
Our technical person is not here to show the overlay of the map, but...

**[01:18:09.800] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001391)  
Babalik ba siya? Darating ba siya later in the hearing?

**[01:18:13.280] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001392)  
Usually, pag-budget hearing, the technical people are here.

**[01:18:16.920] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001393)  
Bakit wala?

**[01:18:18.220] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001394)  
We did that into the mapping.

**[01:18:20.760] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001395)  
We need the data on the most vulnerable areas, and pag-visual, mas nakakatulong, pati sa subcommittee.

**[01:18:29.720] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001396)  
So, that can be read into the record.

**[01:18:32.380] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001397)  
Sige, ma'am, we'll wait for that during this hearing.

**[01:18:34.160] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001398)  
So, let's go on to questions to the MWRO.

**[01:18:40.280] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001399)  
Sino po?

**[01:18:43.800] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001400)  
Chief Regulator T.

**[01:18:45.260] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001401)  
Yes.

**[01:18:45.500] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001402)  
Okay, sir.

**[01:18:47.240] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001403)  
So, moving from DNR...

**[01:18:50.760] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001404)  
Ano naman naghahanda para sa Super El Nino, yung mga malalaking water concessionaires ng MWSS, so, Maynilad and Manila Water?

**[01:18:58.840] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001405)  
Yes, thank you, Mr. Chairman, Mr. Secretary.

**[01:19:01.760] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001406)  
With the MWSSRO actually started preparations and planning for an El Nino last 2022.

**[01:19:07.000] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001407)  
We have a presentation here, if you'll allow us to present the significant figures of what we have done.

**[01:19:13.660] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001408)  
Yes, please proceed.

**[01:19:14.580] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001409)  
Thank you.

**[01:19:15.160] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001410)  
The experience of 2026 provides perhaps the clearest illustration of why continued investment,

**[01:19:20.760] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001411)  
innovation, and effective regulation are so important to our water sector.

**[01:19:24.020] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001412)  
Last July, Angat Dam reached 150 meters, the lowest level ever recorded.

**[01:19:31.380] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001413)  
The previous record low was at 157 meters, a level that would normally result in water service interruptions.

**[01:19:39.200] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001414)  
Despite reaching this historic low, we did not experience a water crisis.

**[01:19:43.340] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001415)  
This was not by chance.

**[01:19:44.820] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001416)  
It was made possible by the timely implementation of critical water projects

**[01:19:49.060] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001417)  
combined with operations in the MWRO.

**[01:19:50.760] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001418)  
The MWSSRO has made substantial adjustments and mitigating measures undertaken by the concessionaires

**[01:19:53.840] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001419)  
in coordination and monitored by the MWSS Regulatory Office.

**[01:19:57.660] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001420)  
First, on the supply augmentation, Manila Water has started several projects

**[01:20:02.540] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001421)  
and several projects since the 2022 rate rebasing, and several have already been delivered.

**[01:20:08.240] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001422)  
This includes the 50 MLD East Bay Phase 1 water treatment plant and the 15 MLD Marikina Portable treatment plant.

**[01:20:15.900] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001423)  
We are also maximizing existing facilities, including the optimization of the Cardona,

**[01:20:20.760] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001424)  
Water Treatment Plant that sources water from Laguna Lake and the Calauis Treatment Plant

**[01:20:25.500] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001425)  
that is sourcing water from the Upper Wawa Dam.

**[01:20:28.340] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001426)  
This is the first dam since the 1960s that was delivered for portable water in Metro Manila.

**[01:20:34.620] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001427)  
And in the coming months, additional capacity is expected from East Bay Phase 2,

**[01:20:40.020] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001428)  
200 MLD by the month of October, and the Kaisaka Treatment Plant with 220 MLD by November 2026.

**[01:20:47.080] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001429)  
These projects demonstrate an important principle, water security,

**[01:20:50.760] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001430)  
cannot depend on a single source.

**[01:20:52.760] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001431)  
We need multiple sources, multiple treatment facilities, and multiple options that can be activated depending on the actual conditions.

**[01:21:02.660] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001432)  
The same approach is being undertaken by Manila in the West Zone.

**[01:21:07.000] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001433)  
Next slide, please.

**[01:21:10.200] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001434)  
This includes the Anabu, Julian, and Molino Modular Treatment Plants,

**[01:21:14.200] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001435)  
which provides additional treatment facilities, particularly in the province of Cavite.

**[01:21:18.680] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001436)  
Manila is also developing alternative water treatment plants, which are also being developed in the West Zone.

**[01:21:20.660] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001437)  
This includes administrative water sources through its new water projects.

**[01:21:23.460] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001438)  
In Paranaque, treated waste water will undergo further treatment to produce

**[01:21:28.840] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001439)  
portable water that can be delivered to Manila customers.

**[01:21:31.720] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001440)  
This is an important development because it allows us to look beyond traditional raw water sources

**[01:21:37.100] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001441)  
and make use of technologies that can provide additional and more resilient sources of supply.

**[01:21:42.000] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001442)  
The new water programme will also be expanded through additional facilities in Valenzuela and Pasay in the coming months.

**[01:21:48.060] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001443)  
I would like to also raise that this new water supply programme will be expanded in the next two years,

**[01:21:50.040] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001444)  
It will be expanded to additional facilities in Valenzuela and Pasay in the coming months.

**[01:21:50.580] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001445)  
facility of manila of manila is actually the first in asia to do direct portable resource reuse

**[01:21:57.300] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001446)  
so at the same time uh my net is also continuing to optimize its upgrading its

**[01:22:01.700] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001447)  
southern treatment plants including potato one two and population to help offset reduced production

**[01:22:08.020] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001448)  
from the lamesa treatment plants so our strategy is not simply to find one new source of water

**[01:22:12.660] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001449)  
it is to develop several sources and maximize the available option next slide please

**[01:22:17.300] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001450)  
next time please second water security is not only about producing more water it's also about

**[01:22:26.100] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001451)  
managing the water we already have that is why operational adjustments and effective

**[01:22:30.580] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001452)  
regulations become critical both concessionaires are implementing pressure management through

**[01:22:35.220] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001453)  
pressure regulating valves and controllers to ensure that available water is distributed as

**[01:22:40.340] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001454)  
efficiently and equitably as possible they are also using real-time monitoring and demand projections

**[01:22:47.300] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001456)  
of reservoir levels. This allows

**[01:22:49.260] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001457)  
them to respond to current demands while maintaining

**[01:22:51.260] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001458)  
sufficient storage for the following day.

**[01:22:54.800] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001459)  
Third,

**[01:22:55.980] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001460)  
non-revenue water.

**[01:22:57.220] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001461)  
I think the Madam Chair

**[01:22:59.480] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001462)  
was mentioning about non-revenue water.

**[01:23:01.880] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001463)  
Non-revenue water is one of the areas

**[01:23:03.460] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001464)  
where we can recover water that is

**[01:23:05.300] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001465)  
already being produced but is

**[01:23:07.060] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001466)  
being lost before it reaches consumers.

**[01:23:09.780] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001467)  
For Manila water, the target

**[01:23:11.320] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001468)  
is to maintain non-revenue water

**[01:23:13.260] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001469)  
below 15%. It is already at

**[01:23:15.300] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001470)  
the international level since the

**[01:23:17.180] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001471)  
target for international is really

**[01:23:19.020] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001472)  
20%. For Manila,

**[01:23:21.280] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001473)  
while non-revenue water is still at

**[01:23:23.060] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001474)  
29%, this has been reduced

**[01:23:25.340] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001475)  
from 44% last

**[01:23:27.220] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001476)  
2021 to just

**[01:23:28.940] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001477)  
29% today. This represents

**[01:23:31.120] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001478)  
a significant reduction in a few

**[01:23:32.760] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001479)  
years. We're

**[01:23:35.240] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001480)  
talking about just a three-year reduction of

**[01:23:36.820] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001481)  
more than 10%,

**[01:23:38.880] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001482)  
which resulted in the recovery of

**[01:23:41.100] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001483)  
nearly 500 MLD.

**[01:23:43.160] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001484)  
In simple terms, every reduction

**[01:23:45.040] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001485)  
in physical water losses means more

**[01:23:47.000] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001486)  
water that can actually be delivered to

**[01:23:48.800] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001487)  
consumers without having to develop

**[01:23:50.680] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001488)  
an entirely new source.

**[01:23:52.620] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001489)  
Next slide, please.

**[01:23:55.300] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001490)  
Fourth, we are also

**[01:23:56.720] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001491)  
looking at other options that we can

**[01:23:58.780] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001492)  
do to help our efforts

**[01:24:00.720] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001493)  
in case that

**[01:24:02.420] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001494)  
existing supplies may not be sufficient.

**[01:24:05.240] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001495)  
This includes maintaining

**[01:24:06.760] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001496)  
emergency resources such as

**[01:24:08.760] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001497)  
mobile water tankers and static water

**[01:24:10.800] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001498)  
tanks, as well as rain catchers and additional

**[01:24:12.820] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001499)  
reservoir capacities. All of these

**[01:24:14.760] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001500)  
measures point to one objective,

**[01:24:16.840] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001501)  
resilience. We are ensuring that

**[01:24:18.660] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001502)  
Metro Manila will not suffer any

**[01:24:20.560] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001503)  
water service interruption or any water

**[01:24:22.520] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001504)  
crisis. That is the commitment that we made

**[01:24:24.520] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001505)  
in 2022, and that is the commitment

**[01:24:26.460] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001506)  
that we intend to keep. Thank you.

Manila Bay,

**[01:29:14.640] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001652)

**[01:29:17.040] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001653)  
No. There's no one that's

**[01:29:19.160] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001654)

Montalban, Antipolo?

It's covered by

**[01:29:29.400] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001659)

**[01:29:31.340] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001660)

Surprising lang na walang

**[01:29:38.940] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001664)  
uptakers pa.

**[01:29:42.140] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001665)  
Kailangan ba

**[01:29:42.780] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001666)  
nagdagdag na impormasyon mula sa,

**[01:29:44.640] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001667)

**[01:29:47.200] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001668)  
need at saka yung

**[01:29:48.760] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001669)

**[01:29:52.840] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001671)

**[01:29:56.120] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001673)  
to that location. So, it's actually

**[01:29:58.540] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001674)  
going to be very expensive.

**[01:30:00.000] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001675)

**[01:30:06.360] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001676)

**[01:30:28.140] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001677)

**[01:30:44.640] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001678)

**[01:30:58.140] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001679)  
So, mga kadaloy, mga nakarating yung tubig sa mga bahay.

**[01:31:01.880] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001680)  
Okay.

**[01:31:02.360] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001681)

**[01:31:04.760] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001682)  
Madam Chairman, Mr. Secretary.

**[01:31:06.760] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001683)

**[01:31:14.540] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001684)

**[01:31:20.760] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001686)  
Yeah. Environmental charge.

**[01:31:23.140] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001687)
