**[01:31:28.020] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001688)  
So, I don't know.

**[01:31:28.140] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001689)  
So, aside from the bulk water bill, meron kang surcharge.

**[01:31:31.360] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001690)

**[01:31:38.560] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001691)

**[01:31:42.680] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001692)

**[01:31:47.240] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001693)

**[01:31:54.900] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001694)  
Sorry. The environmental charge is basically the...

**[01:31:57.480] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001695)

**[01:32:03.880] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001696)  
So, that's why actually every residential customers of Manila Water and Maynilad are entitled to dislodging services once every five years.

**[01:32:12.780] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001697)

**[01:32:16.880] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-001698)  
And that is covered by the environmental charge. It also covers for their watershed protection.

**[01:32:22.380] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001699)  
Well, since...

**[01:32:23.740] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001700)

The trajectory ng department and mga attached agencies.

**[01:32:30.680] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001702)

**[01:32:34.700] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001703)

**[01:32:38.980] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001704)

**[01:32:54.240] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001707)  
Some additional questions sa NWR.

**[01:32:57.480] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001708)  
Dr. David, paano po ima-manage ng NWRB yung nakaambang pressure tungkol sa pagtaas ng presyo, ng singil or rates ngayong posibleng kumonte yung water supply kumpara sa demand?

**[01:33:18.720] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-001709)  
Yes, thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:33:20.800] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-001710)  
With the permission of the Secretary.

**[01:33:22.720] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-001711)  
Yun naman pong NWRB is niregulate po yung rates.

**[01:33:26.400] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-001712)  
But di po natin...

**[01:33:27.480] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-001713)  
Pwedeng direct ang ikonekta ngayon sa nakaambang El Nino.

**[01:33:31.560] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-001714)  
But ang focus po ng NWRB ngayon is how to provide, yung anticipate saan po yung magkakaroon ng issue on deficit or possible supply issue.

**[01:33:41.340] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-001715)  
But focus on how water can be made available to these areas without directly connecting it sa ngayon po, dun sa rates po.

**[01:33:53.520] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001716)  
Siguro nga po.

**[01:33:54.720] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001717)  
Pero syempre, nasa isip yun ng mga...

**[01:33:57.480] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001718)  
constituents nyo at mga consumers ng tubig.

**[01:34:01.040] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001719)  
Paano magsusurvive na kulang nga yung tubig pero tataas pa yung rate.

**[01:34:06.080] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001720)  
Parang it's kontra sa dapat na relief na hinahanap nila.

**[01:34:13.900] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001721)  
Kung by ordinary operation of the market, syempre mas konting supply, mas mahal.

**[01:34:18.040] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001722)  
But double whammy yun sa mga consumers.

**[01:34:20.880] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001723)

**[01:34:23.240] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001724)  
I-follow up ko sa board ninyo.

**[01:34:25.740] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001725)  
I'm sure may colleagues sa plenary.

**[01:34:29.140] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001726)  
And ano po yung ginagawa ng NRWB sa ngayon, NWRB, sa susunod na taon, 2027, para itulak yung pagkakaroon ng bagong water sources?

**[01:34:41.320] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001727)  
Kung mayroon po kayong gustong idagdag sa napag-usapan na at dagdagan din yung utilization ng existing water sources.

**[01:34:48.900] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-001728)  
Yes, thank you Madam Chair.

**[01:34:50.560] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-001729)  
Sa ngayon po Madam Chair, we are working with WRMOO on the various...

**[01:34:55.740] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-001730)  
feasibility studies that were prepared through WRMOO, particularly yung mga development po ng mga new sources.

**[01:35:05.280] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-001731)  
And likewise, may mga projects po ang NWRB, yung mga water resource assessment ng mga major river basins,

**[01:35:13.640] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-001732)  
and part of that assessment, yung identification po ng mga possible sources na pwede pong matap,

**[01:35:21.940] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-001733)  
particularly development ng mga impounding system.

**[01:35:24.980] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-001734)  
Kasi napaka-importante.

**[01:35:25.740] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-001735)  
Napaka-importante po sa ngayon, Madam Chair, na maging climate resilient yung mga river basins,

**[01:35:31.060] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-001736)  
and part of the strategy is the development ng mga impounding system that can be a bit more multipurpose po ang beneficial effect po.

**[01:35:42.260] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001737)  
And nabanggit din nga po kanina yung rainwater catchment facilities, lalo na sa mga LGUs na maraming taon nang mandated to construct and operate those facilities.

**[01:35:55.740] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001738)  
Mandated by law.

**[01:35:58.820] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001739)  
Speaking of the WMRO, WMRO is executing a 100 million peso direct grant facility targeting unserved and island water systems via the local water districts.

**[01:36:14.500] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001740)  
Saan-saan po yan gagawin? At anong mga specific activities at items po ba ang pupondohan ng grant facility na iyon, Administrator Salongka?

**[01:36:23.900] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001741)  
Madam Chair.

**[01:36:25.740] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001742)  
In partnership with the WRMO, we are identifying these water districts na viable or at least qualified to enter into this, to receive these grants.

**[01:36:37.700] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001743)  
Tinitinan po kasi namin, minsan magbibigay ka doon, wala namang community tatanggap at mag-maintain, lalo na magbumukhang desalination facility po yan, magkakaproblema po tayo.

**[01:36:48.420] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001744)  
So baka masayang. So gagawa kami ng criteria, which water districts ang pupwedeng tumanggap.

**[01:36:55.740] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001745)  
To maintain and operate these systems. Kasi parang masasayang lang natin kung maglalagay lang tayo ng facility doon at hindi mapapatakbong mabuti.

**[01:37:05.480] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001746)  
Tinatapos na namin po ni Yusek Rodriguez yung qualifications po niyan.

**[01:37:10.320] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001747)  
So nasa qualification setting pa lang kayo, hindi nyo pa na-identify yung mga locality?

**[01:37:14.640] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001748)  
For, may previous na po, for 2027, lalagyan namin ng qualifications para siguradong magagamit yung mga facilities.

**[01:37:22.500] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001749)  
So i-update nyo lamang po for next year?

**[01:37:25.740] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001750)  
Yes, Madam Chair.

**[01:37:26.540] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001751)  
Sa ngayon, ilang mga lugar ang kinalalagyan itong mga island water systems sa local water district?

**[01:37:37.520] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001752)  
Madam Chair, previously hindi kami privy. Ngayon lang kami magko-coordinate regarding yung matters ng qualifications.

**[01:37:45.420] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001753)  
But we will get back to you doon sa nalagyan ng WRMO.

**[01:37:49.340] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001754)  
Please do. Sino ba dati ang privy sa data na yun? Kung hindi kayo?

**[01:37:53.600] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001755)  
Si Yusek David.

**[01:37:55.740] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001756)  
Si Yusek David, the former Yusek. Okay. And then, habang ina-update nyo yung listahan ng saan-saan gagawin ito, may specific activities at items na ba na tukoy nyo na isasama sa grant facility na ito for next year?

**[01:38:15.960] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001757)  
Madam Chair, since ang targeted niya is island, that could be deep well construction or desalination facilities.

**[01:38:23.440] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001758)  
Okay.

**[01:38:25.740] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001759)  
Depende po sa location.

**[01:38:30.980] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001760)  
Sige.

**[01:38:31.780] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001761)  
Madam Chair, may may. Madam Chair.

**[01:38:33.800] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001762)  
Yes, Yusek. I mean, Sekuna.

**[01:38:35.780] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001763)  
Yeah. The existing structures, I went to a few as I mentioned earlier, in relation to what Administrator Joyce Salonga said.

**[01:38:46.820] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001764)  
So, they're either the barangay or a people's organization, an association in the area.

**[01:38:53.440] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001765)  
Randy, you know.

**[01:38:54.600] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001766)  
And charge a minimum.

**[01:38:55.740] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001767)

**[01:39:05.300] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001768)  
Madam Chair, in relation to the map you were mentioning earlier, we are ready to present now.

**[01:39:10.940] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001769)  
Yes.

**[01:39:11.400] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001770)  
Great. Tamang-tama. Before going on to other concerns with these agencies, please, let's backtrack to that.

**[01:39:17.240] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001771)  
Yung map overlay na ginawa nyo para identify yung three levels of vulnerability and projected

**[01:39:25.740] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001772)  
impact by the Super El Ni?o,

**[01:39:29.220] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001773)  
please proceed Yusek, Analisa.

**[01:39:31.040] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-001774)  
Madam Chair, ma'am, may I introduce Ms. Villa Estolero to present the map?

**[01:39:37.720] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001775)  
Parang presiding officer namin on your mind. Parang I read Estolero. Estolero ba? Escudero?

**[01:39:43.800] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-001776)  
Estolero po, Madam Chair.

**[01:39:45.780] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001777)  
Okay. So, please proceed, Ms. Villa.

**[01:39:49.100] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-001778)  
Ay, ma'am.

**[01:39:55.740] DENR Via Lynelle Estolero** (asr-001779)  
Thank you.

**[01:40:25.720] DENR Via Lynelle Estolero** (asr-001780)  
While the map is waiting to be flashed, I will explain the methodology of how the most vulnerable barangays were assessed based on available spatial data.

**[01:40:38.140] DENR Via Lynelle Estolero** (asr-001781)  
So first of all, we have three available data when we weighted criteria po ng mga most vulnerable sa drought.

**[01:40:54.560] DENR Via Lynelle Estolero** (asr-001782)  
We have the DOST Pag-asa Drought Projections by December 2026 and the Groundwater Stressed Areas from NWRB based on their latest available data of 2025.

**[01:41:09.600] DENR Via Lynelle Estolero** (asr-001783)  
And the Water Service Provider Data based on NWRB Listahang Tubig 2025, DILG Subay Bayan, and available WRMO data.

**[01:41:22.460] DENR Via Lynelle Estolero** (asr-001784)  
So these three factors were weighted.

**[01:41:26.500] DENR Via Lynelle Estolero** (asr-001785)  
So for groundwater stress, there's a score of 1 for groundwater stress and 0 for non-groundwater.

**[01:41:33.300] DENR Via Lynelle Estolero** (asr-001786)  
Water service coverage, 1 if there are no water service providers in the area and 0 if it is covered by a water service provider.

**[01:41:43.400] DENR Via Lynelle Estolero** (asr-001787)  
And the Pag-asa Drought Outlook based on the classification of DOST Pag-asa.

**[01:41:49.500] DENR Via Lynelle Estolero** (asr-001788)  
Drought for 3, dry spell for 2.

**[01:41:52.460] DENR Via Lynelle Estolero** (asr-001789)  
Dry condition 1, then not affected 0.

**[01:41:55.400] DENR Via Lynelle Estolero** (asr-001790)  
Then the scores were summed to determine the priority category.

**[01:41:58.340] DENR Via Lynelle Estolero** (asr-001791)  
So high priority with a total score of greater than 3.

**[01:42:02.360] DENR Via Lynelle Estolero** (asr-001792)  
Moderate priority with a total score of equal to 2.

**[01:42:06.160] DENR Via Lynelle Estolero** (asr-001793)  
Low priority with a total score of equal to 1.

**[01:42:08.620] DENR Via Lynelle Estolero** (asr-001794)  
And not affected with a total score of 0.

**[01:42:11.260] DENR Via Lynelle Estolero** (asr-001795)  
So based on the spatial assessment and GIS analysis of the Geospatial Database Office,

**[01:42:17.620] DENR Via Lynelle Estolero** (asr-001796)  
we have come up that there are 14,434 barangays.

**[01:42:22.460] DENR Via Lynelle Estolero** (asr-001797)  
Or 34% of the barangays nationwide, which are deemed high priority.

**[01:42:27.040] DENR Via Lynelle Estolero** (asr-001798)  
But do note that this assessment is indicative only and based on available spatial data.

**[01:42:33.540] DENR Via Lynelle Estolero** (asr-001799)  
And that's how we come up.

**[01:42:34.780] DENR Via Lynelle Estolero** (asr-001800)  
And for moderate priority, 17,046 barangays, 41%.

**[01:42:40.300] DENR Via Lynelle Estolero** (asr-001801)  
Low priority, 9,685, 23%.

**[01:42:44.020] DENR Via Lynelle Estolero** (asr-001802)  
And not affected, or 883 barangays, or 2%.

**[01:42:48.460] DENR Via Lynelle Estolero** (asr-001803)  
But there is a specific...

**[01:42:52.460] DENR Via Lynelle Estolero** (asr-001804)  
Disclaimer that this is based on the drought data of Pag-Asa forecast for December 2026.

**[01:43:00.340] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001805)  
Thank you so much, Ms. Via.

**[01:43:02.940] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001806)  
So itong pag-identify niyo unang-una ng 14,000 high priority barangays,

**[01:43:08.740] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001807)  
or 34% ng mga barangays sa ating bansa.

**[01:43:12.140] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001808)  
So naka-align na po ba sa mga ito,

**[01:43:16.040] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001809)  
yung mga Super El Nino preparation and response programs,

**[01:43:22.460] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001810)  
activities and projects ng department in the budget so far?

**[01:43:30.340] DENR Via Lynelle Estolero** (asr-001811)  
For Madam Chair, for that information, we would have to confirm.

**[01:43:35.000] DENR Via Lynelle Estolero** (asr-001812)  
But for the available data, we have several projects for WRMO,

**[01:43:41.220] DENR Via Lynelle Estolero** (asr-001813)  
which was overlaid to determine which of these projects directly intersect these most affected areas.

**[01:43:52.460] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001814)  
And which projects are these?

**[01:43:54.620] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001815)  
Tapos sapat na po ba yung naka-appropriate na amounts para sa mga projects na ito?

**[01:44:01.420] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001816)  
Or should I ask WRMO?

**[01:44:06.440] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001817)  
Thank you, Ms. Via.

**[01:44:08.060] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001818)  
Yes, Usec Rodriguez.

**[01:44:09.860] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001819)  
Yes.

**[01:44:11.140] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001820)  
Yes, good afternoon, Madam Chair.

**[01:44:13.300] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001821)  
Good afternoon, Senator Cayetano.

**[01:44:16.020] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001822)  
Ma'am, if I may backtrack as to the types of projects and how this actually evolved.

**[01:44:21.080] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001823)  
Ma'am, the projects that were chosen were as early as 2024. And so this is not really geared towards the El Niño. It just so happens there's El Niño now. But that being said, I think whatever projects that are implemented will still apply to future El Niños because the cycle is from two to seven years, though we cannot really predict when they are. So even if certain projects are not implemented, let's say by December this year in order

**[01:44:52.460] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001849)  
To address the brunt of the El Nino, which is from January to March and probably even extend.

**[01:45:00.000] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001850)  
that for the future El Ninos, we have it there.

**[01:45:03.660] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001851)  
So, ma'am, the types of projects that we have would be first in coordination and collaboration with the LUA

**[01:45:12.260] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001852)  
is the service coverage expansion of water services.

**[01:45:16.940] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001853)  
Basically po, ito po yung mga water district na yung kanilang,

**[01:45:22.140] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001854)  
because there's a limit as to where their pipes are, to where they distribute their water or source capacity.

**[01:45:34.160] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001855)  
So, what we do is we actually grant financial assistance.

**[01:45:38.000] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001856)  
For 2027, the total amount allocated would be 124,092,000.

**[01:45:50.700] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001857)  
Other than this,

**[01:45:52.140] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001858)  
we have the development and implementation of water-related projects.

**[01:45:57.300] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001859)  
Ma'am, if I may, Madam Chair, just to explain,

**[01:46:00.860] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001860)  
actually, when it comes to water systems, it's not just water districts.

**[01:46:07.200] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001861)  
Actually, equally the same number are water systems operated by LGUs.

**[01:46:13.980] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001862)  
So, they're both around 500, if I'm not mistaken.

**[01:46:17.200] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001863)  
So, the beneficiaries of the projects of the WRMO are both

**[01:46:22.140] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001864)  
water districts and LGUs.

**[01:46:24.260] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001865)  
So, when it comes to LGUs, the beneficiaries,

**[01:46:30.460] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001866)  
initial identified beneficiaries are yung mga remote island barangays

**[01:46:34.900] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001867)  
and those other barangays which are considered as isolated.

**[01:46:38.600] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001868)  
Insofar as water districts are concerned, part of the criteria really is yung water districts po na

**[01:46:45.220] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001869)  
plus four, three and four,

**[01:46:46.460] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001870)  
oh,

**[01:46:48.240] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001878)  
class C and D.

**[01:46:50.800] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001879)  
So, the types of

**[01:46:51.820] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001880)  
projects,

**[01:46:54.160] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001881)  
I think it was mentioned already by the Secretary,

**[01:46:56.000] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001882)  
our water filtration program,

**[01:46:58.040] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001883)  
which includes desalination or fresh water.

**[01:47:01.120] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001884)  
We have water refilling stations,

**[01:47:02.720] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001885)  
water tanks for water districts and LGUs,

**[01:47:05.380] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001886)  
and yung geo-resistivity survey.

**[01:47:08.600] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001887)  
So, the total number of water-related projects that we're looking at the target for 2027

**[01:47:15.300] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001888)  
would be 180.

**[01:47:21.820] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001889)  
Itong 180 projects, addressing both water districts and LGUs, tama po ba?

**[01:47:35.320] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001890)  
That's correct, Madam Chair, but mostly water districts.

**[01:47:38.120] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001891)  
Mostly water districts.

**[01:47:39.240] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001892)  
And then itong mostly water districts na i-implementahan nyo ng projects,

**[01:47:45.120] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001893)  
kasama na sila at pati yung LGU-run water systems,

**[01:47:50.380] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001894)  
kasama na sila dun sa 14.

**[01:47:51.820] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001895)  
thousand barangays na sinabi ni Ms. Villa na high priority,

**[01:47:56.160] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001896)  
34% of our barangays in the country.

**[01:48:03.280] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001897)  
Ma'am, what actually we're looking at since the final list of the 2027 beneficiaries

**[01:48:09.720] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001898)  
have not yet been identified, we will include this.

**[01:48:12.740] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001899)  
Ma'am, because I think this is also an important map in the sense that

**[01:48:18.460] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001900)  
for every El Nino cycle, sila rin po lagi ang tinatamaan.

**[01:48:21.820] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001901)  
So, it's a very important map kasi dun sa sinabi niya,

**[01:48:27.220] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001902)  
baka itong mga Masbate City, Calapan City, Isabela Cluster, Cagayan Valley,

**[01:48:32.520] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001903)  
baka chronically naapektuhan ng El Nino, not just this Super El Nino.

**[01:48:39.540] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001904)  
Tamag po yun, Madam Chair.

**[01:48:40.840] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001905)  
In fact, we've actually seen modelings that they've done of previous El Nino years

**[01:48:47.520] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001906)  
na kung saan po nagkaroon ng drought,

**[01:48:49.200] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001907)  
na it always comes back to all the same.

**[01:48:51.820] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001908)  
And yung follow-up question, sa unang-unang question kanina,

**[01:48:58.780] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001909)  
so, aling mga programa ang kailangan dagdaga ng pondo sa ngayon

**[01:49:03.520] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001910)  
in order to address, either to support the 180 projects

**[01:49:08.120] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001911)  
or to address the LGUs and or, sorry, the water districts and or LGU-run water projects

**[01:49:17.440] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001912)  
dito sa 34% of barangays, 14,000 barangays.

**[01:49:21.820] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001913)  
So, that is a high priority.

**[01:49:24.740] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001914)  
Yes, Administrator Salonga.

**[01:49:27.000] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001915)  
Madam Chair, sa experience namin sa LUA,

**[01:49:30.820] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001916)  
ang mga dapat nagdaga ng pondo, yung water source development,

**[01:49:35.580] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001917)  
NRW recovery, saka yung network expansion po.

**[01:49:40.420] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001918)  
Yung network expansion po kasi, hagip yan pareho.

**[01:49:43.780] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001919)  
You can add more water sources or new piping.

**[01:49:47.060] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001920)  
Kaya, ganun po yung nakikita.

**[01:49:49.540] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001921)  
And it's the same request that we get from,

**[01:49:51.820] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001922)  
from water districts.

**[01:49:53.460] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001923)  
Ang worry lang namin sa LUA, kasi nga, kami nagpapautang kami.

**[01:49:57.760] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001924)  
With El Nino, hindi naman lahat ng water districts e-qualified umutang sa amin

**[01:50:02.700] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001925)  
or mapapautang po namin.

**[01:50:04.400] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001926)  
So, ang hinihingi ng water districts is a direct investment of government.

**[01:50:08.840] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001927)  
So, grants po yun.

**[01:50:10.360] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001928)  
So, ang pakiusap namin, kung madadagdagan in whatever means possible sa budget,

**[01:50:16.620] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001929)  
mabigyan ng subsidy po yun.

**[01:50:18.100] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001930)  
And kami naman po, ma-identify naman namin,

**[01:50:21.020] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001931)  
kasi nasa amin yung,

**[01:50:21.820] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001932)  
lahat ng hiling nila.

**[01:50:23.220] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001933)  
And we can give it to the Senate for scrutiny, kung tama.

**[01:50:27.780] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001934)  
Oo. So, I guess, dun sa paggamit yun ng salitang,

**[01:50:30.900] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001935)  
kung pwedeng dagdagan,

**[01:50:32.300] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001936)  
this means, itong tatlong item na in-identify nyo,

**[01:50:34.760] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001937)  
water source development, NRW, saka network expansion,

**[01:50:39.100] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001938)  
sa current form ng budget,

**[01:50:41.080] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001939)

**[01:50:44.000] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001940)  
Madam Chair, sa budget po ng LUA,

**[01:50:47.460] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001941)  
29.5 million pesos lang po yung binigay sa amin.

**[01:50:51.820] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001942)  
Isang water district lang po yun.

**[01:50:54.700] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001943)

**[01:50:57.380] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001944)

**[01:51:00.540] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001945)

**[01:51:01.960] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001946)

**[01:51:05.920] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001947)

**[01:51:10.400] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-001948)  
Kasi yung budget is for next year.

**[01:51:14.200] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001949)  
Yun na nga, may napag-usapan din tayo kanina, no?

**[01:51:16.860] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001950)

**[01:51:20.180] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001951)

**[01:51:21.820] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001952)

**[01:51:25.740] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001953)

**[01:51:32.980] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001954)

**[01:51:44.300] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001958)

**[03:21:32.740] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005280)  
kanina, yung slide

**[03:21:34.780] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005281)  
nung may pamagat siyang

**[03:21:37.440] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005282)  
spatial risk overlay

**[03:21:38.940] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005283)  
identifies 34%

**[03:21:41.600] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005284)  
of barangays as priority

**[03:21:43.180] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005285)  
intervention zones.

**[03:21:45.360] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005286)  
So, baka pwede nyo rin i-address yung slide

**[03:21:47.300] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005287)  
na ito, Sir T.,

**[03:21:49.100] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005288)  
sa pagsagot sa tanong.

**[03:22:24.000] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005289)  
Ready?

**[03:22:26.520] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005293)  
Thank you, Madam Chairperson.

**[03:22:28.640] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005294)  
Yes, based on our report, based on our investigation, we found out that individualized, out of the subdivisions, of the total of 847 in Manila Water, 568 are individualized connections already, and 279 are still on bulk selling.

**[03:22:49.780] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005295)  
For Maynilad customers, 1,351 are individualized, and those are not yet connected is 247 are still under bulk selling.

**[03:23:06.700] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005296)  
So those are the figures right now.

**[03:23:09.800] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005297)  
Actually, there is an existing plan right now to coordinate with both Manila Water and Maynilad to require them to individualize as much as possible.

**[03:23:19.780] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005298)  
However, they are very limited by the situation of the ground, particularly the rights of the private ownership who will refuse to turn it over.

**[03:23:32.060] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005299)  
So those are the biggest issue that the two concessions normally face, that the private developer will not turn it over to them, or that the quality of the pipes and the infrastructure developed by the private developer is not up to speed.

**[03:23:48.740] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005300)  
So.

**[03:23:49.780] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005301)  
But there are always processing right now.

**[03:23:53.300] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005302)  
There are a lot of individual applications right now that are being reviewed, that are being ongoing individualization, and a lot of are under negotiations or under investigations.

**[03:24:05.900] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005303)  
Sa 34% ng baranggays natin na priority intervention zones, nalolocate nyo ba dito any of those communities na may ganitong problema as you just described?

**[03:24:19.160] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005304)  
Yung paano?

**[03:24:19.780] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005305)  
Paano babalansihin or babanggain yung rights of private ownership?

**[03:24:23.320] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005306)  
Tapos yung access sa life-sustaining water ng lahat ng baranggay?

**[03:24:27.500] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005307)  
Yes.

**[03:24:27.720] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005308)  
These are all inside the concession area or limited to Metro Manila and Rizal and province of Cavite.

**[03:24:35.800] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005309)  
So the numbers that I have provided, these are all inside that area.

**[03:24:40.540] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005310)  
And I think there's no major issue with regard to connection because majority, the ones that I mentioned that are not individualized,

**[03:24:49.780] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005311)  
are being serviced by bulk water supply, by Manila Water or Maynilat.

**[03:24:54.260] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005312)  
So water is provided to them.

**[03:24:56.840] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005313)  
So kahit yung mga area na baka may tension between private ownership of the water tapos yung need, hindi naman po sila kasama dito sa 34% of baranggays?

**[03:25:07.260] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005314)  
I cannot answer that because, again, it's limited only to Metro Manila.

**[03:25:13.040] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005315)  
I think majority are outside, the red zones are outside Metro Manila.

**[03:25:16.720] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005316)  
But again, Madam Chair,

**[03:25:19.780] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005317)  
with regard to the issues of whether or not there were some issues with the HOA, the private developer and the residents,

**[03:25:28.080] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005318)  
I can actually confirm that there are issues.

**[03:25:31.880] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005319)  
There are still issues.

**[03:25:33.100] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005320)  
However, our hands are quite tied behind our backs.

**[03:25:38.880] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005321)  
We could not interfere.

**[03:25:40.840] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005322)  
Majority of the issues are with the DSUD and there's pending issues there.

**[03:25:44.720] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005323)  
Like for example, there is a recently, there was actually a request for us,

**[03:25:48.280] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005324)  
and we were trying to assist.

**[03:25:49.760] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005325)  
And however, the DSUD came out with a decision that the current board that we are talking to is not the correct board.

**[03:25:59.500] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005326)  
So the original board was retained.

**[03:26:04.100] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005327)  
So there was an issue of which HOA association that should be discussing with us.

**[03:26:10.160] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005328)  
Siguro, sir, before the plenary, you can just double check, if you may, and inform the subcommittee kung meron nga.

**[03:26:17.960] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005329)  
Hopefully, wala.

**[03:26:19.760] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005330)  
Pero kung meron nga dito sa communities that we're talking about that fall among the 34%.

**[03:26:25.500] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005331)  
At least, you don't think so?

**[03:26:26.820] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005332)  
I don't think so.

**[03:26:27.300] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005333)  
Because Metro Manila is always connected.

**[03:26:30.480] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005334)  
Majority are connected by individualized.

**[03:26:34.140] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005335)  
But in the situation that we're discussing, they're connected by bulk selling.

**[03:26:37.900] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005336)  
So they have bulk water.

**[03:26:38.780] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005337)  
So I don't think they'll be covered by that any drought risk.

**[03:26:43.540] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005338)  
Because they will be provided water.

**[03:26:46.000] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005339)  
That's good to know.

**[03:26:47.500] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005340)  
Of course,

**[03:26:48.960] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005341)  
mga iwan sa atin,

**[03:26:49.760] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005342)  
then paano gawa ng paraan yung 34% outside of these better situated areas.

**[03:26:55.160] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005343)  
Pero nasa top priorities nyo.

**[03:26:56.880] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005344)  
And if there's anything that we could follow up with DSUDE,

**[03:27:02.000] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005345)  
para naman dito sa particular na pinag-uusapan natin,

**[03:27:05.240] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005346)  
please let the subcommittee know also.

**[03:27:07.280] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005347)  
Para if there's anything na pwedeng ipatulong dun sa sponsor naman ng DSUDE budget

**[03:27:12.840] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005348)  
by way of problem solving.

**[03:27:15.040] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005349)  
Yes. Noted.

**[03:27:15.600] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005350)  
Appreciate the detail.

**[03:27:16.060] MWSS Chief Regulator Patrick Lester Ty** (asr-005351)  
Noted, Madam Chairman.

**[03:27:17.040] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005352)  
Alright. Salamat po.

**[03:27:18.520] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005353)  
Some questions now for the mic.

**[03:27:19.760] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005354)  
Mines and Geosciences Bureau.

**[03:27:22.200] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005355)  
Dr. MGB.

**[03:27:23.700] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-005356)  
Yes, sir.

**[03:27:24.200] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005357)  
Director Herades.

**[03:27:27.560] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005358)  
So, gaya ng sinimulan kong banggitin, Director, nung briefing natin.

**[03:27:32.980] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005359)  
Simula po nung 2025, last year, nakatanggap yung opisina ko ng reklamo

**[03:27:38.820] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005360)  
ukol sa intermittent uncontrolled burning ng wooded areas.

**[03:27:44.540] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005361)  
Malapit po sa mga nickel mining areas or tenements.

**[03:27:48.340] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005362)  
Particular, halimbawa sa Manikani Island, sa Giwan, Eastern Samar.

**[03:27:54.000] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005363)  
At protected area po itong Manikani.

**[03:27:57.100] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005364)  
So, ano po yung formal na action so far or plano ng MGB

**[03:28:01.340] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005365)  
para maiwasan yung forest fire doon sa Manikani at sa iba pang wooded areas sa ating bansa?

**[03:28:11.180] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-005366)  
Meron pong program ang mga mining company na magkaroon po ng emergency response team.

**[03:28:16.480] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-005367)  
Pinasama na po namin sa kanila ang training on prevention of forest fires

**[03:28:22.820] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-005368)  
and training ng mga on actual action when forest fires happen.

**[03:28:29.080] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-005369)  
At kasi po ang natural vegetation po ng mga nickel mines,

**[03:28:37.140] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-005370)  
madali pong matuyo sila, lalong-lalo na sa panahon po ng summer.

**[03:28:43.320] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-005371)  
So, meron pong mga accidental.

**[03:28:46.480] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-005372)  
Either po na incidental yung nagtatapon po ng post or talaga pong nag-spontaneous fire po

**[03:28:57.520] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-005373)  
yung mga dried up mga vegetation doon sa area.

**[03:29:03.760] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-005374)  
So, we included po sa training po ng mga ERT ng mga mining company

**[03:29:07.760] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-005375)  
yung action on forest fires po sa mga emergency response team nila po.

**[03:29:14.600] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005376)  
Pero bukod po sa mining,

**[03:29:16.240] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005377)  
mga mining companies mismo,

**[03:29:17.720] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005378)  
ang MGB, ano po yung action ninyo or plano bilang kayo yung may regulatory function sa mga kumpanyang ito?

**[03:29:27.140] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-005379)  
Yes po. Sinasama po namin sila sa mga work program po na ina-approve namin

**[03:29:32.520] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-005380)  
which is being committed by the mining company to for the implementation po.

**[03:29:40.000] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005381)  
Okay. I can appreciate that already, Director.

**[03:29:42.420] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005382)  
Pero baka the Bureau needs to do more.

**[03:29:46.240] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005383)  
Siyempre, kayo ang mas may interest na mapigilan itong mga forest fires na mining related

**[03:29:56.320] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005384)  
at lalo na sa mga protected areas kesa pa in all fairness.

**[03:30:00.000] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005385)  
mga kumpanya. Kasi siyempre,

**[03:30:01.880] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005386)  
yung appetite for risk nila,

**[03:30:04.160] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005387)  
kahit at the risk of forest fire,

**[03:30:05.880] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005388)  
ay siyempre mas mataas

**[03:30:07.380] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005389)  
kesa sa MGB, which is the Bureau of the Department

**[03:30:10.040] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005390)  
of the Environment pa rin.

**[03:30:19.780] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005391)  
Paano po yung gagawin naman

**[03:30:21.760] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005392)  
ng MGB

**[03:30:23.080] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005393)  
para nga paghandaan yung impact

**[03:30:25.780] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005394)  
ng Super El Niño

**[03:30:27.180] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005395)  
sa major mining sites

**[03:30:29.480] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005396)  
sa bansa at sa mga

**[03:30:31.320] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005397)  
komunidad na malapit doon, yung mga mining

**[03:30:33.240] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005398)  
affected communities?

**[03:30:35.920] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005399)  
Yes, will it?

**[03:30:37.560] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-005400)  
Yes, Madam Chair,

Director. Since

**[04:06:44.640] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006268)  
Respond to them in writing before the plenary debate.

**[04:06:47.860] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-006269)  
Thank you very much.

**[04:06:48.880] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-006270)  
We will respond comprehensively in writing, Mr. Aparente.

**[04:06:53.560] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-006271)  
Pero offhand, tutulong po kami na kausapin yung proponent regarding yung request for the, yung data po na sinasabi ninyo.

**[04:07:06.940] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-006272)  
Kakusapin po namin kung pwedeng ipabahagi kung anong available na datos na yung hinihingi po ninyo.

**[04:07:14.640] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-006273)  
Para makita ninyong clearly.

**[04:07:16.100] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-006274)  
I mean, offhand, yun ang, I can assure you, tutulong po kami dyan, Madam Chair.

**[04:07:20.620] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-006275)  
As far as the ECC issue ones is concerned naman, maybe Director Mike Matias can expound on this.

**[04:07:28.560] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-006276)  
But offhand, as far as I know, there was a public consultation that was conducted for this.

**[04:07:38.960] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-006277)  
Pero maybe the extent, I really don't know.

**[04:07:44.640] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-006278)  
So, Attorney Mike, meron ba tayong...

**[04:07:47.320] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006279)  
Salamat, Sec. Puna.

**[04:07:49.380] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006280)  
Yung dun sa commitment nyo na tutulong kayong kausapin yung proponent para maibahagi yung datos.

**[04:07:56.360] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006281)  
In particular, binanggit ni Mr. Aparente yung computer-run files, yung mathematical models, at dalawa pang items.

**[04:08:04.440] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006282)  
Yes, Director Matias, meron ba kayong brief na response?

**[04:08:08.180] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006283)  
And or could you also respond to Bulacan Alliance for Reform in Writing?

**[04:08:14.020] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006284)  
Thank you.

**[04:08:14.640] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-006285)  
Thank you, Madam Senator and Sir.

**[04:08:17.740] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-006286)  
Based on the EIA process, it went through the scoping and the public participation, which is a requirement.

**[04:08:25.180] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-006287)  
And given yung request, we'll coordinate with the release of the information.

**[04:08:32.360] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-006288)  
Kasi, Madam Senator, I think naka-anchor yung way forward dun sa information being asked by Sir Aparente.

**[04:08:44.640] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006289)  
Salamat, Director Matias.

**[04:08:46.400] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006290)  
Hopefully, maging opening po yan, kabukasan po yan para makarating sa isang maayos na sagot dun sa tanong nila, Mr. Aparente, na paanong magagawang paraan dito sa sitwasyong ito.

**[04:09:03.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006291)  
Salamat po muli, Mr. Aparente.

**[04:09:06.180] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006292)  
Mag-keep in touch po tayo sa subcommittee, papunta po sa budget plenaries para sa iba nyo pang follow-up questions.

**[04:09:14.640] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006293)  
At maaring mga proposed amendments for the consideration ng subcommittee in partnership sa department.

**[04:09:22.840] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006294)  
Maraming salamat.

**[04:09:23.940] Bulacan Alliance for Reform Representative Avelino P. Aparente** (asr-006295)  
Salamat din po.

**[04:09:25.340] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006296)  
Any other brief highlights na gusto nyong ibahagi from Civil Society?

**[04:09:31.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006297)  
Mr. A.J. Montesa sa People's Budget Coalition.

**[04:09:34.940] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006298)  
Yes. Maraming salamat po.

**[04:09:36.660] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006299)  
Good afternoon, Madam Chair, Senator Hontiveros, DNR officials, and to our colleagues from Civil Society.

**[04:09:42.220] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006300)  
On behalf of the People's Budget Coalition,

**[04:09:44.640] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006301)  
Ken Amante and myself, I would like to thank you, Sen. Teresa, for inviting us as resource persons.

**[04:09:51.200] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006302)  
I think we do need to acknowledge that this is a huge step.

**[04:09:54.300] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006303)  
This is the first time that civil society is being invited to a department budget hearing in at least 10 years.

**[04:10:00.540] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006304)  
So we really do acknowledge how important this proceeding is for us and for restoring trust in the entire budget process after the damage that was done in the previous scandals.

**[04:10:11.940] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006305)  
For the DNR budget, we believe that there is no shortage.

**[04:10:14.620] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006306)  
There is no such thing as a natural disaster.

**[04:10:16.700] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006307)  
All disasters are man-made.

**[04:10:18.840] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006308)  
Failures in planning, coordination, enforcement, and watershed protection make things worse.

**[04:10:24.720] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006309)  
And the flood control scandal of the last years showed billions of pesos spent on infrastructure while communities remain dangerously vulnerable.

**[04:10:33.220] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006310)  
So we look to the DNR.

**[04:10:34.480] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006311)  
We expect an agency that will protect our watersheds, enforce environmental laws, scrutinize development projects,

**[04:10:42.140] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006312)  
and ensure that government investments do not aggravate

**[04:10:44.620] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006313)  
the very disasters that they are supposed to prevent. Our assessment is that the current version of the DNR budget will not adequately prepare us for typhoons, flooding, urban heat, and the extreme weather conditions worsened by the strong El Nino.

**[04:10:59.800] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006314)  
The budget of the DNR Office of the Secretary has been stuck at 16 to 20 billion pesos since 2018.

**[04:11:05.820] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006315)  
And accounting for inflation over the last 10 years, and even the recent depreciation of the peso and oil price hikes,

**[04:11:13.240] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006316)  
this means that the real budget...

**[04:11:14.620] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006317)  
of the department has fallen by about 30% in real terms.

**[04:11:18.980] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006318)  
Capital outlays fell from 37% in 2018 to just 12% for this coming year.

**[04:11:24.680] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006319)  
And 1.8 billion pesos was left unobligated in 2025.

**[04:11:29.620] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006320)  
In the 2027 proposal, the Environmental Management Bureau, or EMB,

**[04:11:33.880] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006321)  
which issues and monitors environmental compliance certificates,

**[04:11:37.140] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006322)  
is the only attached bureau that was cut or reduced by 360 million pesos.

**[04:11:41.640] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006323)  
So we want a stronger DNR that...

**[04:11:44.620] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006324)  
enforces our environmental laws to prevent these disasters.

**[04:11:48.400] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006325)  
But a bigger budget will not necessarily make communities safer

**[04:11:52.280] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006326)  
if the DNR keeps approving projects without studying these projects comprehensively.

**[04:11:58.240] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006327)  
To be frank, our civil society movements are very concerned

**[04:12:01.300] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006328)  
that DNR too often acts as a rubber stamp in its environmental impact assessment procedures.

**[04:12:08.500] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006329)  
Feedback on the ground has been that the department proposes certifications and approvals

**[04:12:12.680] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006330)  
without sufficiently accounting for the impact of the environmental impact.

**[04:12:14.620] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006331)  
So we need an agency that anticipates harm and not just one that merely responds

**[04:12:22.800] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006332)  
after the damage has already been done.

**[04:12:25.520] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006333)  
So we illustrate three systemic issues using three cases.

**[04:12:29.160] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006334)  
First, the environmental...

**[04:12:30.620] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006335)  
Briefly na lang, yung tatlo.

**[04:12:33.080] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006336)  
Either enumerate the three or detail one, please.

**[04:12:37.080] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006337)  
Thank you.

**[04:12:37.760] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006338)  
The first one is the environmental clearance of expressway projects,

**[04:12:42.300] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006339)  
especially the SALEX,

**[04:12:43.600] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006340)  
which is San Miguel's 40.65-kilometer Southern Access Link Expressway

**[04:12:49.300] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006341)  
na sasagasa sa mga puno, kalikasan, paaralan, ospital, negosyo at heritage

**[04:12:55.220] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006342)  
sa Quirina Rojas Boulevard, Padre Faura, Buendia, C3R10, and Shoreline Expressway.

**[04:13:01.740] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006343)  
The different civil society movements like Stop SALEX and Ilog Pasiglahin movements

**[04:13:06.560] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006344)  
have pointed out the bad impacts of elevated expressways

**[04:13:09.940] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006345)  
on health, traffic, heritage, environment, and economy.

**[04:13:13.600] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006346)  
So we ask, why is the DENR approving elevated expressways

**[04:13:17.620] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006347)  
even when the most progressive cities in the world are tearing them down?

**[04:13:22.160] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006348)  
So ang tanong po namin, why did the construction of a 40-kilometer expressway

**[04:13:26.260] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006349)  
proceed without a full environmental impact assessment?

**[04:13:29.700] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006350)  
Why did it proceed with only three initial environmental examination checklists

**[04:13:34.380] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006351)  
covering a total of only 13 kilometers?

**[04:13:37.280] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006352)  
I think for the other two issues, I think my colleague Ken will just briefly touch on them po.

**[04:13:42.180] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006353)  
Thank you.

**[04:13:43.600] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006354)  
AJ, yes, Ken Abante from People's Budget Coalition. Briefly, please.

**[04:13:47.960] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006355)  
Maraming salamat po, Sen. Teresa.

**[04:13:49.300] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006356)  
Yung pangalawang karanasan po na gusto namin i-share ay sa tingin po namin napakahalaga

**[04:13:53.380] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006357)  
para po sa preparation natin sa El Ni?o at pagbaha.

**[04:13:56.300] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006358)  
Ito po yung karanasan sa sistema ng watershed restoration.

**[04:14:00.100] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006359)  
Yung Upper Marikina Basin Protected Landscape covering 26,000 hectares in Rizal

**[04:14:05.140] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006360)  
ay protection po natin sa matinding pagbaha at tagtuyot sa buong Kamainilaan.

**[04:14:09.580] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006361)  
Pero po, despite its protected status, the watershed remains severely

**[04:14:13.600] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006362)  
degraded.

**[04:14:14.840] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006363)  
Yung forest cover po nito ay 25% na lamang against DENR's restoration target of 43% by 2028,

**[04:14:23.080] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006364)  
which we believe to still be too low.

**[04:14:25.680] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006365)  
Dapat 72% to 90% ayon sa isang 2018 World Bank DENR study.

**[04:14:30.980] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006366)  
The higher the forest cover, the greater the water and disaster resilience benefits versus river runoffs.

**[04:14:36.380] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006367)  
Ang status po ng restoration, 1,500 of these 26,000 hectares are in danger of incursion

**[04:14:43.600] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006368)  
2 major projects need urgent review and finalization by the DENR.

**[04:14:48.760] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006369)  
Ang una, si Rapid City at sister company nitong Quarry Rock

**[04:14:52.320] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006370)  
ay may quarrying projects na kinansinalanan ng DENR noong 2022.

**[04:14:56.760] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006371)  
Maraming salamat po DENR.

**[04:14:58.440] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006372)  
Pero hindi pa rin po finalized ang desisyon

**[04:15:00.000] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006373)  
sa kanilang motion for consideration, which potentially impacts 985 hectares.

**[04:15:06.680] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006374)  
Ang pangalawang proyekto po ay ang VINA Energy na may application naman

**[04:15:10.720] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006375)  
para sa isang 553 hectares na special use agreement in protected areas or SAPA

**[04:15:16.300] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006376)  
at pending pa rin po ito sa DNR.

**[04:15:18.700] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006377)  
At may formal opposition na po dito ang Upper Marikina Watershed.

**[04:15:23.220] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006378)  
So we hope, isa lang pong kaso ang Upper Marikina Watershed.

**[04:15:26.780] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006379)  
We think this has to be one of the core issues that are discussed by the committee

**[04:15:30.800] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006380)  
and also in the plenary because we want prevention, not just damage control.

**[04:15:36.820] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006381)  
And we also ask DNR to account for all the other watersheds in the country

**[04:15:41.140] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006382)  
because we are hearing from different civil society groups that this is also the case.

**[04:15:45.840] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006383)  
And finally po, we think that DNR has a responsibility to also stop the flood control scandal.

**[04:15:50.940] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006384)  
Ayon po sa isang Action Klima Report, only 0.1% of the national budget

**[04:15:56.520] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006385)  
is spent on nature-based solutions.

**[04:15:59.560] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006386)  
Karamihan po nang natag sa climate budget ay puro mga DPWH gray infrastructure.

**[04:16:05.100] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006387)  
So we ask the DENR to please help us review the 7,054 projects worth 190 billion pesos

**[04:16:12.680] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006388)  
that we tagged were inserted by the House of Representatives without proper documentation.

**[04:16:18.760] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006389)  
We ask for the Senate's help on this.

**[04:16:21.220] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006390)  
We have the list. We have the maps.

**[04:16:23.840] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006391)  
We are seeing many projects in many different areas.

**[04:16:27.120] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006392)  
That were inserted by the House without proper documentation.

**[04:16:30.900] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006393)  
Please DNR, please help us make sure that these projects are proper.

**[04:16:36.400] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006394)  
And just to summarize po, we hope that the committee supports us in the review of these expressway projects

**[04:16:42.900] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006395)  
and the incursions in the Upper Marikina Watershed and other watersheds

**[04:16:46.740] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006396)  
to publish the status and work with us on verifying all of these maps

**[04:16:52.460] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006397)  
and making sure that the scientific community is really part of the process,

**[04:16:56.520] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006398)  
of approving the projects.

**[04:16:58.360] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006399)  
We are with you.

**[04:16:59.760] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006400)  
Hindi na po natutulog ang aming mga angry nerds.

**[04:17:01.900] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006401)  
So pag-check po ng 18,000 projects that were passed by the House.

**[04:17:07.700] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006402)  
And we are committed, Senator Riza and Secretary Cuna,

**[04:17:11.180] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006403)  
to work with you on guarding our public money.

**[04:17:16.700] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006404)  
Thank you.

**[04:17:18.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006405)  
Ken Abante also from the People's Budget Coalition.

**[04:17:22.840] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006406)  
The subcommittee hears you too.

**[04:17:25.920] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006407)  
And...

**[04:17:26.520] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006410)  
Very well noted yung mga initial na follow-up questions or points that you have raised.

**[04:17:34.600] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006411)  
And gaya ng sinabi ko rin kay Mr. Aparente,

**[04:17:37.600] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006412)  
asahan po namin ang subcommittee in partnership sa DNR

**[04:17:41.200] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006413)  
yung patuloy niyong engage sa amin papunta sa plenary.

**[04:17:46.920] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006414)  
Both for additional questions tulad ng dinagdag niyo ngayong hapon

**[04:17:52.860] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006415)  
at saka possible proposed amendments.

**[04:17:56.400] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006416)  
I think very exciting yung possibilities dito.

**[04:18:00.880] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006417)  
So let's do our best.

**[04:18:03.640] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006418)  
So I would like to thank, marami salamat po ang DNR at ang attached agency sa pagpresenta nyo ng budget para sa 2027.

**[04:18:15.680] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006419)  
Important issues ang nag-surface kasama na yung kakulangan ng paghahanda natin sa dumating na at tumitindi pang Super El Nino.

**[04:18:25.360] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006420)  
So...

**[04:18:25.560] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006421)  
So...

**[04:18:26.280] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006422)  
Asahan po ninyo na makikipagrabaho ang subcommittee sa department

**[04:18:31.560] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006423)  
at saka sa finance committee chair para i-transform yung DNR budget sa isang El Nino ready budget

**[04:18:39.720] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006424)  
para ma-mitigate yung negatibong epekto sa ating kalikasan at sa ating mga kababayan.

**[04:18:45.400] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006425)  
Senator?

**[04:18:46.160] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006426)  
Yes.

**[04:18:46.700] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006427)  
Ay ma'am, sorry. There was another...

**[04:18:49.400] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006428)  
Hala!

**[04:18:51.160] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006429)  
You should have said so earlier. That's what your mic is for.

**[04:18:54.340] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006430)  
Thank you.

**[04:18:55.040] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006431)  
Thank you again.

**[04:18:55.720] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006432)  
Okay.

**[04:18:56.160] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006433)  
Briefly na lang please, Ms. Eunice of the Mangrove Breakthrough.

**[04:19:00.960] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006434)  
Mangrove Breakthrough.

**[04:19:02.480] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006435)  
Yes. Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[04:19:03.320] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006436)  
Two minutes please.

**[04:19:04.100] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006437)  
Very briefly.

**[04:19:05.100] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006438)  
So I think our colleagues at the PBC have already talked about the upstream issues on watershed management.

**[04:19:10.360] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006439)  
I'd like to focus on downstream to coastal communities.

**[04:19:14.260] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006440)  
So I'm Eunice Tanilon.

**[04:19:15.500] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006441)  
Thank you so much to the subcommittee, Secretary Kuna, and our colleagues from DNR.

**[04:19:19.460] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006442)  
I'm Eunice Tanilon of the Mangrove Breakthrough, a global partnership of government, scientists, and investors

**[04:19:24.760] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006443)  
working to secure the world's mangroves by 2030.

**[04:19:28.340] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006444)  
Our partner is the Global Mangrove Alliance Philippines, comprised of key conservation organizations.

**[04:19:35.980] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006445)  
We work together with the DNR and the National Blue Carbon Action Partnership.

**[04:19:39.960] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006446)  
So it's essentially a roadmap for five years on how to restore our key mangrove priority landscapes.

**[04:19:49.720] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006447)  
So I think what I want to talk about this afternoon is to really just...

**[04:19:52.960] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006448)  
how difficult it's been to put together financing and money for the very large-scale coordination that's needed

**[04:20:01.960] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006449)  
to support restoration projects.

**[04:20:05.920] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006450)  
Ms. Eunice, briefly please.

**[04:20:07.440] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006451)  
Kasi may pagkakataon pa tayo na mag-usap at makipagtrabaho.

**[04:20:10.820] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006452)  
Please proceed.

**[04:20:12.240] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006453)  
But please wind up.

**[04:20:13.180] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006454)  
I think to jump straight to our recommendations, we really hope that the DNR could fund,

**[04:20:19.040] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006455)  
and of course the committee could fund the foundations,

**[04:20:22.160] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006456)  
and put a date on protocol.

**[04:20:23.660] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006457)  
So we would really like a national carbon stock assessment carried out with our universities,

**[04:20:28.660] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006458)  
funded through the research lines,

**[04:20:30.660] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006459)  
and a commitment that a blue carbon quantification protocol will be issued as a department order

**[04:20:36.660] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006460)  
by the end of the first quarter of 2027.

**[04:20:39.160] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006461)  
This has been the key item that's been blocking all of our conversations with investors.

**[04:20:45.160] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006462)  
We've been in conversations with ADB, AIIB, and also other banks in Singapore.

**[04:20:51.160] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006463)  
And a lot of...

**[04:20:52.080] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006464)  
a lot of our conversations stalled because of the lack of these foundational items.

**[04:20:56.580] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006465)  
And secondly, we hope that a special provision in the DNR budget that accounts for blue carbon

**[04:21:02.080] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006466)  
separately from forest carbon requires the department to designate within 90 days

**[04:21:08.080] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006467)  
the bureau accountable for mangroves inside and outside protected areas

**[04:21:12.080] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006468)  
and requires an annual public hectare count against the PSA mangrove accounts.

**[04:21:17.580] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006469)  
So we really hope with this that it gives every loan, grant, and private peso

**[04:21:21.580] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006470)  
a public share to match against.

**[04:21:24.580] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006471)  
So we're very willing to help the Mangrove Breakthrough and Global Mangrove Alliance on these items.

**[04:21:29.580] Makati Business Club Governance and Sustainability Lead Eunice Tanilon** (asr-006472)  
Thank you very much.

**[04:21:31.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006473)  
Thank you very much too, Mangrove Breakthrough.

**[04:21:36.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006474)  
Yung blue carbon niyo very interesting kasi dinidinig nga namin yung blue economy bill

**[04:21:42.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006475)  
sa Committee on Economic Affairs.

**[04:21:44.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006476)  
And special provision, we love special provisions in the budget.

**[04:21:49.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006477)  
What is that?

**[04:21:51.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006478)  
Platform towards making them regular line items in the following year's budget.

**[04:21:56.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006479)  
So, salamat, salamat sa inyo.

**[04:21:59.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006480)  
I really share the excitement to, sabi ko nga kay SEC nung briefings namin,

**[04:22:04.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006481)  
to raise the profile of the department strategically in the years ahead.

**[04:22:09.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006482)  
Starting, continuing now, especially dito sa panahon ng Super El Nino.

**[04:22:14.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006483)  
And for the department to have more naman than 1% of the budget.

**[04:22:21.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006484)  
0.1, oh my God, okay, 0.1% of the budget and to spend it optimally.

**[04:22:29.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006485)  
Anyway, so to continue, I was just going to close our hearing for today.

**[04:22:35.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006486)

**[04:22:42.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006487)

**[04:22:45.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006488)
